| **Approved screen** | **Global Search** — [410](architecture/410-approved-ui-specification.md) |
| **Module** | **Product Surface — Lane A**, reading `knowledge_graph/public/` |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE**, 2026-08-10, on `main`. **Previously:** **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane A**, 2026-08-06. ✅ **Shipped the same day it was claimed, and it turned out to be a repair.** ⚠️ **[B-208](BUGS.md): Global Search crashed the moment it found anything.** The endpoint has returned typed groups since [TASK-408](#task-408--b-ii--search-results-are-grouped-and-the-ranking-is-explainable) and this surface read them as flat results, so `row.account` was `undefined` and the dropdown threw on the first match. **Three gates missed it and each was reasonable alone** — `tsc` saw a type parameter on a JSON call, which is a claim rather than a check; the frontend test mocked the hook with the pre-TASK-408 shape; the backend test flattens groups deliberately. **What nothing owned was the seam.** ✅ **Now renders the grouping the server already sends**, reading `coverage` before `results` so *nothing matched* and *we did not look* stay different sentences. **+4 tests and a corrected mock**, 1444 frontend, `mypy --strict` clean over 797 files, `make validate` PASSED. *(Previously* **`CLAIMED`**, the moment [TASK-456](#task-456--the-signal-catalog-explains-itself-to-a-customer) reached `IN VALIDATION`. ⚡ **Unlike the other approved screens in this queue it needs no entrance ruling**: search already lives in the shell header, and `account-search.tsx` says why in its own words — *"search is a verb, not a place, so this lives in the shell header rather than becoming another navigation entry."* **So [C-229](LANE_COORDINATION.md) does not reach it.** *(Previously* ✅ **`READY`** — **Lane C, 2026-08-06.** **Placed last, reordering nothing**.*)* |
| ✅ **[§0-D](PRODUCT_OFFICE.md)'s four questions, measured before this row was written** | ⚡ **① Reads** — `GET /workspaces/{id}/account-search` and `/account-identities` · **② Workspace-wide?** ✅ **Yes, and verified rather than assumed** — the route is workspace-scoped, ⛔ **not entity-scoped like `TriageQueries.queue`, which is what [C-228](LANE_COORDINATION.md) cost** · **③ Writes** — ⛔ **none, and correctly: a search that writes is not a search** · **④ Entrance** — ✅ **`AccountSearch` is mounted in `components/shell/app-shell.tsx` itself.** ⚠️ **It appears nowhere in `navigation.tsx`, which is why ④ was checked rather than assumed — search is *in* the shell, not an entry *on* it** |
| **Delivers** | **The existing account search raised to the approved specification: typed grouping across the object kinds that exist, and the ranking [TASK-408](#task-408--b-ii--search-results-are-grouped-and-the-ranking-is-explainable) already made explainable** |
| ⛔ **Out of scope, named rather than discovered late** | ⚠️ **A search *index*** — [450](architecture/450-global-search-traceability-audit.md) measured **`meilisearch`, `tsvector` and `trigram` all at zero.** **This slice does not build one**: 450 records a ranking index as **sequenced behind a trigger that has not fired**, and building one for a workspace's account list is the speculative optimisation §14 forbids · **Saved search** — [R-02](DOMAIN_DISCOVERY_REGISTER.md), [TASK-449](#task-449--saved-view--the-publiccontract-and-nothing-else) · ⚡ **Monitored search** — **deliberately *not* R-02**: 450 narrowed the capability by measuring that *a monitored search is a subscription, not a view* · **Natural-language questions** — AI Copilot's, [TASK-453](#task-453--the-six-verbs-become-a-published-contract) · **`EntityKind.PERSON`** — [R-01](DOMAIN_DISCOVERY_REGISTER.md), and **deals were re-classified *out* of `EntityKind` by [450](architecture/450-global-search-traceability-audit.md)** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **Render only.** No endpoint, no index, no migration |
| ⚡ **Playwright journey** ([D-113](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **A seller types a company name into the shell and reaches that account** — ⚡ **[MC-1](PRODUCT_EXPERIENCE.md) calls it *"the first thing every evaluator types"*, so it is the first thing the journey does** |
| ⚠️ **The rule it must not break** | **[P-A](DOMAIN_DISCOVERY_REGISTER.md).** **No results and a failed query must never render alike** — ⛔ *"nothing matched"* and *"we could not look"* are opposite answers to somebody who typed a name they know exists |

### TASK-1400 · The Authenticated Entry Engine — one resolver, twenty-four reason codes

| | |
|---|---|
| 🔴 **Why this is first, and it is platform** | 📁 **[D-193](DECISION_REGISTER.md): *"Treat it as a platform specification, not a feature specification."*** ⚡ **Every workflow from 1 to 28 consumes it.** ⛔ **[D-195](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §4: solve it once in shared infrastructure rather than in each workflow** |
| **Epic** | **Authenticated Entry** — **[workflow 29](coordination/workflows.toml)**, the first of its rows |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** — ⚡ **the resolver is presentational routing, not authorization** ([§20c](../CLAUDE.md): a client check is a courtesy, never enforcement) |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE, 2026-08-10.** ⚡ **Evidence rather than assumption: `git merge-base --is-ancestor` confirms every commit on `lane-a-product-surface` is contained in `main` at `346a0c4`, so this row's work has shipped.** **Was:** ✅ **`VERIFIED` — QE, 2026-08-09, at `14b3985`.** ⚡ **Verified by opening what the row claims, not by reading its status line** ([B-146](BUGS.md)): **24 rules** ✓ · **six gates** ✓ (`1`–`6`, distinct) · **pure** ✓ — *zero* `fetch`, `await` or `async` anywhere in `frontend/lib/entry/**` · **closed type** ✓ — `as const` + indexed access, so a twenty-fifth code is unconstructable · **35 unit tests pass**. ⚠️ **One discrepancy, recorded rather than resolved: the row's *Delivers* says *21 reason codes* and the shipped `REASON_CODES` has 24.** ⛔ **The design snapshot states *twenty-four rules* and is silent on a code count**, so `21` is a preparation figure the implementation refined — **not a deviation from the design authority, and not QE's to rewrite.** ✅ **Zero production callers is the row's own Definition of Done** — *"no caller yet — this row ships the engine and wires nothing"*. *(was: `IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-09)* |
| **Design authority** | ⛔ **`docs/design-snapshot/screens/Fortune Signora Authenticated Entry.dc.html` and nothing else** ([D-138](DECISION_REGISTER.md)). **No checklist, no restatement** ([D-137](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) |
| ⚡ **Delivers** | **`resolveEntry(session, intent) → { route, reason }`** · **the 24 rules in the document's order, first match wins** · **the 24 reason codes as a closed type** ⚠️ *(this row said 21 three times; the design's table carries 24 distinct codes — corrected 2026-08-09 on [C-250](LANE_COORDINATION.md) ①, found by Lane A extracting them from the document rather than reading this row)* · **the six gates** |
| 🔴 **The properties that are the point** | ⛔ **PURE: same inputs, same destination, and NO NETWORK CALL inside the decision** — 📁 **the spec says so in terms.** ⚡ **That makes the whole engine unit-testable without a browser, a session or a database, which is why the test suite is cheap and total** |
| ⛔ **Closed, not open** | **The 24 reason codes are a closed type — a twenty-fifth cannot be added from outside the module.** ⚡ **Same shape as `ReasoningVerb`, which [TASK-453](#task-453) already proved in this repository** |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | ✅ **Everything the engine reads already exists**: session (Better Auth), membership and workspace state (`workspace_tenancy`), onboarding completion. ⛔ **Absent: nothing.** ⚡ **The engine computes, it does not fetch — that is what *pure* buys** |
| **Reusable components required** | ✅ **None — no surface** |
| **Shared layout** | ✅ **n/a** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`frontend/lib/entry/**` and its tests** · 🟠 **nothing** · 🔴 **nothing** — ⚡ **a pure module with no dependency is the cheapest possible first slice of a platform capability** |
| **Playwright coverage** | ⛔ **None — not a customer journey** ([§19](../CLAUDE.md)) |
| **Component coverage** | 🔴 **All 24 rules, table-driven, one case per rule** · ⚡ **plus the property that decides correctness: for every session/intent pair the engine returns exactly one route AND one reason, never a route with no reason** · **and `FELL_THROUGH` is reachable — a fallthrough that cannot occur is [§2](../CLAUDE.md)'s decorative guard** |
| **Definition of Done** | **24 rules, 21 codes, six gates, pure** · ⛔ **no caller yet — this row ships the engine and wires nothing** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `frontend/lib/entry/` · **Routes** none · **APIs** none · **Unit** the 24-rule table · **Security Invariant** ⛔ **the engine must never be the only check — [§20c](../CLAUDE.md), Casbin on the server decides and this chooses a screen** |
| ✅ **Verification strategy** | **The rule table is the verification, and it is derived from the design document rather than restated** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | 🔴 **The temptation is to fetch inside a rule** — *"just look up whether the workspace is archived."* ⛔ **That destroys purity, testability and the diagnostics guarantee at once, and the spec forbids it in one sentence** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **n/a — this is Signora's own domain** |

### TASK-1402 · The intent resolver — what the person was trying to reach

| | |
|---|---|
| **Epic** | **E1 · Entry Engine** — **[workflow 29](coordination/workflows.toml)**, `kind = platform` |
| ⚡ **Why it exists** | 📁 **The spec: *"Authentication hands over an identity AND AN INTENT — the route the person was trying to reach."*** ⛔ **Four of the 24 reason codes are intent codes — `INTENT_HONOURED`, `INTENT_MISSING`, `INTENT_FORBIDDEN`, `INTENT_NOT_MEMBER` — so intent is a first-class input, not a query parameter** |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** — ⛔ **presentational routing, never authorization** ([§20c](../CLAUDE.md)) |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE, 2026-08-10.** ⚡ **Evidence rather than assumption: `git merge-base --is-ancestor` confirms every commit on `lane-a-product-surface` is contained in `main` at `346a0c4`, so this row's work has shipped.** **Was:** 🔵 **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-10.** 📁 **[`lib/entry/intent.ts`](../frontend/lib/entry/intent.ts)** — **capture · carry · classify · discard**, and ✅ **all four codes reachable.** 🔴 **① No open redirect**: the rule is POSITIVE — *one leading `/`, no scheme, no control characters* — ⛔ **not a blocklist**, and it refuses `//evil.example`, `/\evil.example`, an absolute URL, `javascript:`, and `/\njavascript:` — ⚠️ **the last of which has no scheme at the moment it is checked, because the browser strips the newline and then it does.** ⚡ **The same test runs on DECODE**, which is the one that matters: an attacker never calls the capture function, they craft the `?next=`. 🔴 **② `not_member` is decided locally and the server is NEVER asked** — §7's *404, never 403* applied before the question rather than after, ⛔ **because asking would leak through timing what the status code withholds.** ✅ **Both fire-tested: 3 tests red each way.** **21 new tests; 2560 frontend green; tsc 0; eslint 0.** **Was: `CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-10** |
| **Design authority** | ⛔ **`docs/design-snapshot/screens/Fortune Signora Authenticated Entry.dc.html`** ([D-138](DECISION_REGISTER.md)). **No checklist, no restatement** ([D-137](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) |
| ⚡ **Delivers** | **Capture the attempted route before authentication · carry it through the whole flow · classify it into the four intent codes · and DISCARD it safely when it names a workspace the person is not in** |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | ⛔ **Nothing to reuse — no intent survives login today.** 🔴 **`sign-in-form.tsx` pushes to `/` unconditionally, which DISCARDS intent, and that is the defect [TASK-1401](#task-1401--the-five-hardcoded-redirects-become-one-call-site) repairs** |
| **Reusable components required** | ✅ **None — no surface** |
| **Shared layout** | ✅ **n/a** |
| **Manual review gate** | ✅ **n/a — no customer surface.** ⛔ **Stated rather than skipped** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`frontend/lib/entry/**`** · 🟠 **nothing** · 🔴 **nothing** |
| **Component coverage** | **A forbidden intent yields `INTENT_FORBIDDEN` and NOT a 403 page** — ⚡ **the spec routes rather than errors** · **an intent naming a non-member workspace yields `INTENT_NOT_MEMBER`, never a leak of whether that workspace exists** ([§7](../CLAUDE.md): a non-member gets 404, never 403) |
| **Playwright coverage** | ⛔ **None unless stated — not a customer journey** ([§19](../CLAUDE.md)) |
| **Definition of Done** | **All four intent codes reachable, and an intent for a workspace the caller is not in NEVER discloses its existence** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `frontend/lib/entry/` · **Unit** the four classifications · **Security Invariant** 🔴 **intent must not become a workspace-enumeration oracle — asserted, not assumed** |
| ✅ **Verification strategy** | **The four codes are the verification, table-driven from the document** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | 🔴 **An intent is attacker-controlled input.** ⛔ **Honouring it without membership re-verification is precisely [§13](../CLAUDE.md)'s *a client-supplied workspace identifier is never trusted*** — ⚡ **the resolver classifies; the server still decides** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **n/a — Signora's own domain** |

### TASK-1403 · Reason codes become a diagnosable record, not a log line

| | |
|---|---|
| **Epic** | **E2 · Reason codes & diagnostics** — **[workflow 29](coordination/workflows.toml)**, `kind = platform` |
| ⚡ **Why it exists** | 📁 **The spec: the code is *"written to the session, logged with the redirect, and shown to support in the diagnostics bundle — so 'why did I land here' is answerable WITHOUT REPRODUCING THE LOGIN."*** ⚡ **That last clause is the requirement; everything else is implementation** |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** — ⛔ **presentational routing, never authorization** ([§20c](../CLAUDE.md)) |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE**, 2026-08-10, in certified `e71e74d`. **Previously:** 🔵 **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-10.** ✅ **All three the design names, together**: 📁 **[`lib/entry/diagnostics.ts`](../frontend/lib/entry/diagnostics.ts)** — **`entryMark` (the session) · `entryRecord` (the redirect, joinable by `correlationId`) · `diagnosticsBundle` (support) · `explainEntry` (the sentence, naming the gate).** 🔴 **The Security Invariant holds**: **the bundle is workspace-tenanted, the scope is a REQUIRED argument, another workspace's and another person's records never reach it, and what was withheld is COUNTED** — ⛔ **a silent gap gets chased.** ⚡ **A record with no workspace is KEPT**, because gates 1–2 fire before any workspace loads and those are the refusals support is asked about most. ⛔ **Not a second log**: the record is shaped so the [TASK-492](#task-492--the-security-event-stream--one-append-only-record-every-module-writes) stream takes it verbatim, and **the producer that hands it over is outside this row's 🔴 budget and is stated rather than built.** 🔴 **Found and fixed a real defect on the first read — [B-230](BUGS.md)**: ⛔ **every gate 1 and gate 2 decision carried a workspace id**, which is the disclosure the gate ordering exists to prevent. ✅ **16 new tests; 2519 frontend green; tsc 0; eslint 0.** **Was: `CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-10** |
| **Design authority** | ⛔ **`docs/design-snapshot/screens/Fortune Signora Authenticated Entry.dc.html`** ([D-138](DECISION_REGISTER.md)). **No checklist, no restatement** ([D-137](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) |
| ⚡ **Delivers** | **The reason written to the session · the redirect logged with it · and a support-readable bundle** — ⛔ **all three, because any two of them still require reproducing the login** |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | 📁 **`correlation_id` propagation already exists** ([§16](../CLAUDE.md), [§17](../CLAUDE.md)) — ⚡ **the reason joins the record the platform already keeps.** ⛔ **Absent: the session field and the bundle** |
| **Reusable components required** | ✅ **None — no surface** |
| **Shared layout** | ✅ **n/a** |
| **Manual review gate** | ✅ **n/a — no customer surface.** ⛔ **Stated rather than skipped** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`frontend/lib/entry/**`** · 🟠 **a session field — announced in the commit** · 🔴 **nothing** |
| **Component coverage** | **Every redirect emits exactly one reason** · ⛔ **a redirect with no reason FAILS the test** — ⚡ **that is what makes the guarantee real rather than aspirational** |
| **Playwright coverage** | ⛔ **None unless stated — not a customer journey** ([§19](../CLAUDE.md)) |
| **Definition of Done** | **Support can answer *why did I land here* from the bundle alone, without a reproduction** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `frontend/lib/entry/` · **Unit** one reason per redirect, always · **Security Invariant** ⛔ **the bundle must never carry another workspace's data** ([§11](../CLAUDE.md)) |
| ✅ **Verification strategy** | **A support engineer answering the question from the bundle, with no session, is the verification** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | ⚠️ **A diagnostics bundle is a data-egress surface.** ⛔ **It is workspace-tenanted like everything else, and that is stated here rather than assumed because a debug artifact is where tenancy is usually forgotten** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **n/a — Signora's own domain** |

This is the same invention, one register over.** ✅ **`DISPOSED` is the state that already means this** — 📁 **[TASK-903](BACKLOG.md) uses it for exactly this case: *"Lane B delivered the port inside the row that needed it."***  ⛔ **Lane C, 2026-08-09, [D-204](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §6.** ✅ **Verified by content on `origin/integration`, never by a status string** ([C-428](LANE_COORDINATION.md)): **`resolveWorkspace(session, intent)` is at `frontend/lib/entry/rules.ts:82`** and implements this row's four branches in this row's own order — **① the granted intent (rule 06) · ② last-used when healthy (rule 12) · ③ fall through to the next healthy membership BEFORE returning an unhealthy one (rule 14) · ④ a sole membership, healthy or not**. 📁 **`WorkspaceStanding = 'active' | 'suspended' | 'archived' | 'deleted'`** is `session.ts:61`, **with `archived` carrying the read-only case rather than a fifth member** — the design's own rule 16. ⚡ **This row's Definition of Done was *"twelve cases, twelve outcomes"*, and `frontend/tests/unit/entry-engine.test.ts` holds exactly twelve, covering all four standings.** ⛔ **Nothing here is outstanding.** ⚠️ **Left in place rather than deleted so the decomposition stays legible and nobody re-derives it as a gap** — **[TASK-483](#task-483--the-authentication-shell) was claimed and found already built, and this row is what that costs when it is not recorded** |
| **Design authority** | ⛔ **`docs/design-snapshot/screens/Fortune Signora Authenticated Entry.dc.html`** ([D-138](DECISION_REGISTER.md)). **No checklist, no restatement** ([D-137](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) |
| ⚡ **Delivers** | **Sole membership · last-used · user default · and the three unavailable states, each with the route the spec names** — `/w/:ws/archived`, `/w/:ws/suspended`, `/w/:ws/deleted` |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | ✅ **`workspace_tenancy` owns membership and it is already RLS-enforced.** ⛔ **Absent: last-used, a user default, and any notion of archived or suspended** — ⚠️ **those are STATE the platform does not model, so this row adds it** |
| **Reusable components required** | ✅ **None — no surface** |

### TASK-1406 · The card registry — modules contribute capabilities, never navigation

| | |
|---|---|
| **Epic** | **E4 · Home composition** — **[workflow 29](coordination/workflows.toml)**, `kind = platform` |
| ⚡ **Why it exists** | 📁 **The spec's principle, and [D-193](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §5 repeats it: *"modules register capabilities rather than issuing redirects."*** ⚡ **A composition is a filtered, ordered read of a registry — which is what makes a fifth composition free and a sixth module additive** |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** — ⛔ **presentational routing, never authorization** ([§20c](../CLAUDE.md)) |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE**, 2026-08-10, on `main`. **Previously:** ✅ **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-10.** 📁 [`lib/home/cards.ts`](../frontend/lib/home/cards.ts) — **the catalogue, the contract, the composition ordering and the presentational filter.** ⚡ **Built to look like the two registries the row named, and they were READ first**: `policy.csv` and `tenancy_policy.py` are **both static and declarative**, so ⛔ **there is no `registerCard()`** — **runtime registration would make a composition's contents depend on module import order**, which is the defect `REASON_CODES`' comment already warns about. ✅ **A lane contributes by adding a line, which is a diff a reviewer sees.** 🔴 **The discipline copied is `tenancy_policy.py`'s**: *a misclassification is a build failure, never a silent exemption* — **add a `CardId` and forget to place it in a composition and the suite goes red**, ✅ **fire-tested.** ⚡ **Order is the design's, never the registry's** — also fire-tested by making `cardsFor` return registry order, which fails. ⛔ **`REGISTRY` is EMPTY and the emptiness is asserted**: no module publishes a Home card yet, so every composition resolves to nothing — **the `ShellBanners` condition, reported rather than filled with placeholders.** ⚠️ **One function was DELETED after being written and tested**: `unplacedCards()` could never return anything, because every `CardId` is placed by construction — 🔴 **[§2](../CLAUDE.md) Decorative Logic in this lane's own new code**, and the real guard is one level up where it can actually fail. 🔴 **Presentational, never authorization** — the row's named risk: `requires` hides a card the caller could not use and **Casbin still decides** ([§20c](../CLAUDE.md)). **2205 vitest / 108 files (+11, +1) · tsc 0 · eslint 0.** **Was: `CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-10** |
| **Design authority** | ⛔ **`docs/design-snapshot/screens/Fortune Signora Authenticated Entry.dc.html`** ([D-138](DECISION_REGISTER.md)). **No checklist, no restatement** ([D-137](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) |
| ⚡ **Delivers** | **A card contract · a registration mechanism · ordering · visibility · and permission filtering** — ⛔ **filtering is PRESENTATIONAL: Casbin still decides, this only hides what the caller cannot use** ([§20c](../CLAUDE.md)) |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | 📁 **The pattern exists twice already** — `policy.csv` and `tenancy_policy.py` are registries every lane appends to ([§20e](../CLAUDE.md)). ⚡ **This is the third, and it should look like them** |
| **Reusable components required** | ✅ **None — no surface** |
| **Shared layout** | ✅ **n/a** |
| **Manual review gate** | ✅ **n/a — no customer surface.** ⛔ **Stated rather than skipped** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`frontend/lib/home/**`** · 🟠 **the card contract in `components/ui`** · 🔴 **nothing** |
| **Component coverage** | **A module registering a card causes NO change to the composition code** — ⚡ **that is the assertion, and a composition that needs editing to accept a card has failed the design** · **a card the caller may not see is absent, not disabled** |
| **Playwright coverage** | ⛔ **None unless stated — not a customer journey** ([§19](../CLAUDE.md)) |
| **Definition of Done** | **Four compositions read one registry; adding a card touches no composition** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `product_surface` · **Unit** registration is additive · **Security Invariant** ⛔ **a hidden card must not have already fetched its data — visibility is decided BEFORE the read, or hiding is theatre** |
| ✅ **Verification strategy** | **Adding a fifth card without editing a composition is the verification** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | 🔴 **The failure mode is a card that hides itself AFTER fetching.** ⛔ **That leaks exactly what the permission was protecting, and it is invisible in the UI — which is why the invariant is on the read and not on the render** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **n/a — Signora's own domain** |

'/'` is exactly the defect the row names**, and a union makes both outcomes something a caller must handle. ⛔ **The 24 rules are never walked for a machine**: they are questions about a person's account and memberships. 🔴 **Two properties live in the TYPE rather than a comment**: ⛔ **an unscoped machine credential is unconstructable** (495: *no category for a cross-tenant credential*) and ⛔ **a machine principal cannot omit its owning human.** ⚠️ **An impersonated human is still a human and still gets a screen** — `acting_for` marks it, it does not refuse it. ✅ **15 tests; 2575 frontend green; tsc 0; eslint 0.** **Was: `READY` — RELEASED by Lane C, 2026-08-09** |

| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | 📁 **[495](architecture/495-principal-and-identity-boundary.md)'s model is ratified and unbuilt.** ⚡ **This row does NOT build the principal model — it makes the engine's signature admit one, which is a type change while there is exactly one caller** |

| **Epic** | **E3 · Workspace resolution** — **[workflow 29](coordination/workflows.toml)** |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** reading **`workspace_tenancy`**'s `public/` |
| **Status** | ⏸️ **`BLOCKED` — Lane C, 2026-08-09, on [C-1405](LANE_COORDINATION.md): the Workspace Chooser specification does not exist.** 🔴 **THIS ROW WAS RELEASED `READY` EARLIER TODAY AND THAT WAS THIS OFFICE'S ERROR** — ⛔ **it was sized from a rules-table sentence and published to Lane A as *cheap*.** ⚡ **The [D-206](DECISION_REGISTER.md) review is what caught it, which is the review earning its keep.** ✅ **The FINDING that produced this row still stands and is unaffected: `rules.ts` returns `/workspaces` and no such route exists, so [TASK-1401](#task-1401--the-five-hardcoded-redirects-become-one-call-site) still must not be wired.** ⛔ **What changed is who can close it and how much it costs** |
| **Design authority** | 🔴 **NONE — AND THIS ROW CLAIMED ONE IT DID NOT HAVE.** ⛔ **Corrected 2026-08-09 by the [D-206](DECISION_REGISTER.md) review.** ⚠️ **This cell named *`Authenticated Entry` §03 rule 13* — which is ONE SENTENCE IN A RULES TABLE**, not a specification for a screen. 📁 **The Entry Coverage Review's finding: *"The single largest hole. Four rules route to `/workspaces` and no document draws it."* It classifies the chooser **D · major new document**, ~6 sections.** 🔴 **And it names eight things nobody has decided**: search and filtering past ~8 workspaces · recency, pinning and ordering · unavailable memberships shown with state rather than hidden · pending invitations listed alongside memberships · loading, error and empty-organisation states · a keyboard model with no shell to inherit from · responsive behaviour. ⚡ **It also separates this from the App Shell switcher and says why: *the chooser runs BEFORE a workspace context exists — no rail, no search, no assistant, and no workspace-scoped permission to lean on.*** ⛔ **[TASK-1408](#task-1408--the-application-shell--the-frame-six-backlog-rows-are-waiting-for)'s switcher is a different component** |
| ⚡ **Delivers** | **The `/workspaces` route** · **every membership listed, INCLUDING the unavailable ones with their reason** — 📁 the design is explicit that a suspended or archived workspace is *shown with its reason*, not hidden · **and an explicit-switch entry point, because the design says the chooser serves both first login and a deliberate switch** |
| 🔴 **What it must NOT do** | ⛔ **Become the default destination.** 📁 ***"last resort only"*** — **it is rule 13 of 24, and a chooser shown to somebody with one workspace is the design's own failure case** |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | ✅ **`workspace_tenancy` already publishes membership** — ⚡ **[TASK-1408](#task-1408--the-application-shell--the-frame-six-backlog-rows-are-waiting-for)'s switcher reads the same list, so this row and the shell must consume ONE contract.** ⛔ **Two reads of membership that disagree about *available* is the defect this row exists inside** |
| **Reusable components required** | **The membership list is shared with the App Shell's switcher** — **whichever lands first owns it** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`frontend/app/(app)/workspaces/`** |
| **Playwright coverage** | **A person with two healthy memberships reaches the chooser and can enter either** · 🔴 **a suspended workspace is LISTED with its reason and is not enterable** — **the assertion that separates *shown with a reason* from *hidden*** |
| **Component coverage** | **A person with exactly one membership never sees this route** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | **The list is a `<ul>` of links, not clickable divs** · **an unavailable entry is `aria-disabled` with its reason as the accessible description** — ⛔ **never colour alone** |
| 📱 **Responsive behaviour** | **Single column below `sm`; the reason text wraps rather than truncating** — ⛔ **a truncated reason is worse than none** |
| **Definition of Done** | **`resolveEntry` can return `/workspaces` and a person arrives somewhere real** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `product_surface`, `workspace_tenancy` · **Routes** `/workspaces` · **Security Invariant** 🔴 **only the caller's own memberships are listed** — ⛔ **an unavailable workspace's NAME is still tenant data, and the reason must not disclose more than the standing** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | ⛔ **Shipping [TASK-1401](#task-1401--the-five-hardcoded-redirects-become-one-call-site) first.** ✅ **This row is cheap and it removes a guaranteed 404 from the wiring row's blast radius** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **n/a** |

### TASK-1422 · Last-used workspace — stored, read, and cleared when membership ends

| | |
|---|---|
| 🔴 **The authorization hazard [D-196](DECISION_REGISTER.md) named and nobody owned** | 📁 **Recorded at D-196: *"last-used workspace is a stored preference that OUTLIVES membership — if it is not cleared on removal, a departed member's next login routes at a workspace they can no longer see, which is an authorization failure wearing a convenience feature's clothes."*** ⛔ **No row carried it until now** |
| **Epic** | **E3 · Workspace resolution** — **[workflow 29](coordination/workflows.toml)** |
| **Module** | **`workspace_tenancy`** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE, 2026-08-10.** ⚡ **Evidence rather than assumption: `git merge-base --is-ancestor` confirms every commit on `lane-a-product-surface` is contained in `main` at `346a0c4`, so this row's work has shipped.** **Was:** 🔵 **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-10.** 🔴 **There is NO `last_used_workspace_id`, and that is the design rather than an omission.** ⛔ **A pointer is a second representation of a fact the preference rows already hold**, and the two disagree the first time a membership ends — 📁 **which is [D-196](DECISION_REGISTER.md)'s hazard exactly.** ✅ **`person_preferences.last_entered_at` (migration `0047`, reversible, verified down and up) + the answer DERIVED over LIVE memberships** — 🔴 **so a workspace somebody has left is UNREACHABLE rather than stale, and there is nothing to forget to clear.** ⚡ **`ForgetWorkspaceEntry` runs on both ending paths and is documented as HYGIENE rather than sold as the fix.** ⚠️ **The row names three paths; this product has two**: ⛔ **workspace deletion has no use case and the FK already cascades**, and ⛔ **a role change revokes nothing — both roles can see the workspace — so a clear wired there would fire on nothing.** **Recorded, not built.** ✅ **12 tests; `last_used_workspace.py` 100%, `preferences.py` 100%; repository 95.11% against the 95.0% floor; 4375 backend green; mypy strict clean; 37 import contracts kept.** **Was: `CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-10** |
| **Design authority** | ⛔ **`Fortune Signora Authenticated Entry.dc.html` §03 rule 12 and §07** |
| ⚡ **The measured gap** | 📁 **`resolveEntry` READS `session.lastUsedWorkspaceId` — `frontend/lib/entry/session.ts`.** ⛔ **Nothing in the platform WRITES it, and nothing clears it.** ⚡ **The engine's half is correct and complete; this is the other half** |
| ✅ **Delivers** | **A per-person stored preference** · **written on entering a workspace** · 🔴 **and CLEARED when the membership ends — by removal, by workspace deletion, and by role change that revokes access** |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | ✅ **[TASK-470](#task-470--workflow-9--user-profile-to-the-approved-design) shipped `person_preferences` and the `PERSON_OWNED_TABLES` tenancy class** (migrations `0044`/`0045`) — ⚡ **this is a preference of exactly that shape and must not create a second store** |
| **Reusable components required** | ✅ **None — this is backend** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟠 **`backend/modules/workspace_tenancy/**` and a migration** — announced |
| **Playwright coverage** | 🔴 **A person removed from their last-used workspace signs in and does NOT land there** — **the whole row in one assertion** |
| **Component coverage** | **Entering a workspace records it** · **deleting a workspace clears it for every member** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | ✅ **n/a — no surface** |
| 📱 **Responsive behaviour** | ✅ **n/a** |
| **Definition of Done** | **The preference is written, read by the engine, and provably cleared on every path that ends membership** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `workspace_tenancy` · **Security Invariant** 🔴 **the resolver only ever routes to a workspace present in `memberships`** — ✅ **[TASK-1400](#task-1400--the-authenticated-entry-engine--one-resolver-twenty-four-reason-codes) already asserts this over 2,880 session/intent pairs, so a stale preference cannot route anybody; ⛔ but it CAN cost a person their real destination and land them at the chooser, which is a correctness defect rather than a security one.** ⚡ **The distinction is the row's own reasoning and must survive into the tests** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | ⛔ **Clearing on removal only.** ✅ **Three paths end access — removal, deletion, role change — and the design's rule 20 `ROLE_CHANGED` exists because the third is real** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **n/a** |

| | |
|---|---|
| **Classification** ([D-208](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §4) | ⚪ **NOT STARTED** — ⛔ **not legacy, not partial, and not a refinement of anything that exists** |

| **Module** | **`product_surface` — Lane A** *(frontend)* · **`opportunity_engine`** *(reads)* |
| **Status** | ⏸️ **`SEQUENCED`** — ⛔ **NOT `READY`, deliberately.** ⚡ **The prerequisite is named below and is genuinely unmet** |
| **Design authority** | 📁 **[`Fortune Signora Opportunity Intelligence.dc.html`](design-snapshot/screens/)** — ⛔ **read it; this row does not restate it** |
| 🔴 **The measurement** | ⛔ **No route exists.** `find app -type d | grep -iE 'deal|opportunit'` returns nothing. 📁 **The design's breadcrumb is `Deals / EMEA enterprise / Vantage renewal — Q4 FY26`** — **a DEAL record, reached through a Deals destination.** ⛔ **Neither exists** |
| ⛔ **Seven named sections, none present** | **AI win probability vs rep forecast** *(`Commit 90%` against `34% win probability`, `−23 pts`, `gap 56 points`)* · **What moved it** *(contribution bars, centre line is no effect)* · **Deal health** *(five dimensions, equal weight)* · **Opportunity summary** *(`Since Friday · 14 sources`, `Confidence 4 of 5`, `Show reasoning`)* · **Update deal** · **Add to forecast call** · **Ask about this deal** |
| ⚠️ **What IS built, and why it is NOT this screen** | 📁 **`/accounts/[accountId]` mounts nine intelligence components** — `EvidenceHealth · WhyNow · ContributionList · PostureSplit · ReadingValidity · ScoreArithmetic · ContestedSince · OpportunityMomentum · WorkflowStage`. ⚡ **They are conceptually adjacent — contributions, arithmetic, evidence — and they are built around the BUYING SCORE on an ACCOUNT.** ⛔ **The design here is built around WIN PROBABILITY on a DEAL.** 🔴 **Different subject, different aggregate, different page** |
| ✅ **⛔ DO NOT DELETE OR REBUILD THOSE NINE** | 🔴 **This row's most important line.** ⚡ **They are not legacy and not an earlier draft of this screen** — **they are most likely Account 360's** ([`Fortune Signora Account 360.dc.html`](design-snapshot/screens/)), **which is a separate finalized document with its own row, [TASK-1415](#task-1415).** ⛔ **Verify against that document before touching one of them.** ⚠️ **Treating an adjacent screen's correct implementation as this screen's legacy is the failure this row exists to prevent** |
| 🔴 **The unmet prerequisite — why `SEQUENCED` and not `READY`** | ⛔ **A deal record needs a deal.** 📁 **The design reaches it through `Deals`, which is one of the rail destinations [C-269](LANE_COORDINATION.md) records as having no route.** ⚡ **And `opportunity_engine`'s read surface has not been measured against this screen's needs** — **win probability, forecast gap, deal health and the contribution set are four distinct reads, and this office has confirmed none of them.** ✅ **It frees when a Deals destination exists and that measurement is done** |
| ⚡ **What Lane B can do NOW, in parallel** | ✅ **Measure `opportunity_engine`'s seven endpoints against the four reads above and report the gap.** ⛔ **No frontend dependency, no product decision, and it is the thing that turns this row from `SEQUENCED` into `READY`** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`app/(app)/**`** and 🟢 **`components/intelligence/**`** *(new components only)* · 🟠 a new route — announce it |
| **Definition of Done** | **A seller opens a deal and sees why the model disagrees with the forecast** · **certified · `main`** |

### TASK-1468 · Signal Center — the triage model is right and eight surfaces the design draws are missing

| | |
|---|---|
| **Classification** ([D-208](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §4) | ⚡ **MISSING IMPLEMENTATION — additive.** ⛔ **NOT a rebuild, and that is this row's most important line** |
| **Epic** | **Opportunities & Signals** — [C-306](LANE_COORDINATION.md) |
| **Module** | **`product_surface` — Lane A** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`READY` — Lane C, 2026-08-11, from the C-306 design-first reconciliation** |
| **Design authority** | 📁 **[`Fortune Signora Signal Center.dc.html`](design-snapshot/screens/)** — ⛔ **read it; this row does not restate it** |
| ✅ **KEEP the existing implementation — it is not previous-generation** | 🔴 **[C-306](LANE_COORDINATION.md) asks *rebuild or keep*, and the measurement answers KEEP.** 📁 The design's thesis is *"a triage queue, not a notification list · every signal ends in a decision"* — ✅ **`useWorkspaceTriage` implements the queue, and `ResolveOutcome` is exactly the design's four exits: `actioned` · `assigned` · `snoozed` · `dismissed`.** ✅ **`MIN_REASON` implements *"dismissed with a reason"*.** ✅ **The three state tabs — *Needs review · Snoozed · Closed* — are the design's.** ⛔ **Nothing invented was found, and no legacy structure. The implementation is a faithful SUBSET** |
| ⛔ **Missing — eight surfaces, measured** | **① Saved views** (`＋ New view`, each with a count) · **② `＋ Filter` and filter chips** (*"Confidence ≥ 3"*, *"14 of 22 shown"*) · **③ bulk selection** (*"✓ 3 selected"* and its action bar) · **④ `Rules`** destination · **⑤ `Analytics`** destination · **⑥ `Review next ⏎`** — the keyboard triage flow · **⑦ the counts header** (*"14 need review · 6 assigned to you · 41 this week"*) · **⑧ live arrival** (*"Live · 3 arrived while you were reading"*) |
| ⚠️ **Also absent, and it is a behaviour rather than a surface** | 📁 ***"Virtualised · newest signals never reorder the list while it is focused."*** ⚡ **The second half is the load-bearing one** — a list that reorders under a reader's cursor loses the row they were about to action. **Severity (`Critical`) is not displayed either** |
| ✅ **REUSE — ① is already built and this row must not build a second one** | 🔴 **[TASK-452](#task-452) shipped the Saved View store**, and 📁 [455](architecture/455-context-definitions-r02-r03.md) §4 **names Reports among its consumers.** ⚡ **Signals is another consumer of that same store — ⛔ not a new saved-view mechanism.** 📁 **`lib/views/working-list.ts` carries the three refusals; read them before writing a fourth** |
| ⚠️ **Sequencing — split before claiming** | ⛔ **Eight surfaces is not one slice.** ✅ **① · ② · ③ · ⑦ are queue-local and independent.** ⚠️ **④ and ⑤ are DESTINATIONS — [C-269](LANE_COORDINATION.md) applies: the rail entry belongs to the slice that builds the destination, and neither destination exists.** ⚡ **⑥ depends on ③'s selection model.** ⛔ **Take ①–③ and ⑦ first; raise ④/⑤ as their own rows when reached** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`app/(app)/signals/**`** · 🟢 its tests · 🟠 **a `components/ui` addition only if the reuse audit finds none** |
| **Playwright coverage** | **A signal leaves the queue by each of the four exits · a saved view survives navigation · the list does not reorder while a row is focused** |
| **Definition of Done** | **A seller triages from a saved view, filters it, and actions in bulk** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚠️ **Runtime status** | ⛔ **LEVEL 1/2 NOT VERIFIED** — source-derived; no authenticated session |

### TASK-1423 · The six gate screens — a failure that is designed, not a redirect loop

| | |
|---|---|
| 🔴 **The spec's central promise, and nothing implements it** | 📁 **§02: *"each gate depends on the one before it resolving, and a gate that FAILS produces a designed screen."*** ⛔ **Today a failed gate has nowhere to go.** ⚡ **`resolveEntry` returns a `route` and a `reason` for every terminal rule and **not one of those routes exists*** |
| **Epic** | **E2 · Entry states** — **[workflow 29](coordination/workflows.toml)** |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** |
| **Status** | ⏸️ **`BLOCKED` — Lane C, 2026-08-09, on [C-1405](LANE_COORDINATION.md).** 🔴 **Also released `READY` earlier today in error**: ⛔ **eleven gate screens were treated as one row against §08, and the [D-206](DECISION_REGISTER.md) review shows they span FOUR documents, three of which do not exist.** ✅ **Three of the eleven ARE buildable today — `403`, `404` and `/auth/locked` — and if Lane A wants a slice, that is the honest one.** ⚠️ **`/auth/locked` carries its own caveat: the SCREEN is drawn and [C-431](LANE_COORDINATION.md) records that NO LOCKOUT CAPABILITY EXISTS**, so the state is unreachable — ⛔ **which the review classifies `A · buildable today with no design work` and is the one place its vocabulary misleads** |
| **Design authority** | ⚠️ **PARTIAL, and the split is now measured** ([D-206](DECISION_REGISTER.md)). ✅ **Covered today — `403` and `404` are fully drawn in `System States` (rules 07, 08); `/auth/locked` is drawn in `Authentication` (rule 03).** 🔴 **NOT covered — and each needs a document that does not exist**: **`SESSION_EXPIRED` (rule 04) → *Session Continuity*, which the review calls *"the largest true gap in the audit"*** · **`STEPUP_REQUIRED` (rule 05) → *Security Gates*** · **`WS_SUSPENDED` · `WS_ARCHIVED` · `WS_DELETED` (rules 15–17) → *Workspace Lifecycle States*** · **account deleted and suspended (rules 01, 02) → an account-lifecycle section `System States` does not yet have** |
| ⚡ **Delivers a screen per terminal reason** | **`ACCOUNT_LOCKED` · `ACCOUNT_SUSPENDED` · `ACCOUNT_DELETED` · `SESSION_EXPIRED` · `STEPUP_REQUIRED` · `WS_SUSPENDED` · `WS_ARCHIVED` · `WS_DELETED` · `NO_WORKSPACE` · `INTENT_FORBIDDEN` · `INTENT_NOT_MEMBER`** — ⚡ **each states what happened and what the person can do, in the design's own words** |
| 🔴 **Why it is not [TASK-1409](#task-1409--workflow-28--system-states--the-scope-ladder-and-a-failure-that-claims-the-smallest-surface-it-can)** | ⛔ **System States are failures of the SYSTEM — 404, 403, 500.** ⚡ **These are refusals of an ENTRY DECISION, and the person is authenticated.** ✅ **They share the ladder's primitives and say different things; conflating them produces a 403 where the truth is *your workspace is archived*** |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | ⚠️ **`STEPUP_REQUIRED`'s screen is [TASK-1410](#task-1410--step-up-authentication--the-capability-behind-stepup_required)'s challenge** — ⛔ **this row renders the gate, that row makes it enforceable** · ⚠️ **`ACCOUNT_LOCKED` has no lockout capability ([C-431](LANE_COORDINATION.md))** — ✅ **the screen ships and the state is unreachable until it exists, declared rather than faked** |
| **Reusable components required** | **[TASK-1409](#task-1409--workflow-28--system-states--the-scope-ladder-and-a-failure-that-claims-the-smallest-surface-it-can)'s primitives** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`frontend/app/(app)/**`** |
| **Playwright coverage** | 🔴 **Every terminal reason code reaches a rendered screen and none reaches a redirect loop** — **enumerated from `REASON_CODES`, so a twenty-fifth code cannot ship without a screen** |
| **Component coverage** | **Each screen names its reason code in a copyable support identifier** — 📁 the design puts it *"in the support diagnostics bundle"* |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | **Focus moves to the screen's heading** · **`role="alert"` for refusals, `role="status"` for waiting** |
| 📱 **Responsive behaviour** | **Single column below `sm`, actions stacked** |
| **Definition of Done** | **No terminal rule can produce a route that does not render** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Security Invariant** 🔴 **a refusal names the STANDING, never the resource** — **the same rule as [C-245](LANE_COORDINATION.md) and as [TASK-1409](#task-1409--workflow-28--system-states--the-scope-ladder-and-a-failure-that-claims-the-smallest-surface-it-can)'s `403`** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | 🔴 **This is the row that makes [TASK-1401](#task-1401--the-five-hardcoded-redirects-become-one-call-site) safe.** ⛔ **Wiring the engine while eleven of its destinations 404 would be worse than the behaviour it replaces** |

| 🔴 **What it must NOT do** | ⛔ **Rebuild the BACKEND.** 📁 **[TASK-438](#task-438--the-executive-dashboards-five-sections-that-need-nothing-new)'s own title is *the five sections that need nothing new*, and that is true of the reads.** 🔴 **CORRECTED 2026-08-10: this cell said the row *"RE-HOMES working code"*. ⛔ There is no frontend code to re-home. The sections must be BUILT against 📁 `docs/design-snapshot/screens/Fortune Signora Executive Dashboard.dc.html`, which no file in `frontend/` cites** |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | ✅ **The reads are on `main`** — `domain/pipeline_health.py`, the endpoint and its tests, verified by `git ls-tree origin/main`. ⚡ **Zero backend work for four sections.** ⛔ **The fifth — *ICP coverage, in versus outside* — was declared out of scope by TASK-438 and is blocked by [C-332](LANE_COORDINATION.md); this row inherits that, it does not resolve it** |
| **Reusable components required** | **[TASK-1406](#task-1406--the-card-registry--modules-contribute-capabilities-never-navigation)'s card contract** |

This cell said *"the dashboard works — it works as the wrong thing"*.** ⛔ **The dashboard does not work, because it does not exist on screen.** ⚡ **The real risk is the opposite of the one recorded: a lane claiming this row expecting a re-homing job, finding no UI to re-home, and inventing the sections rather than deriving them** — **which is exactly how [`attention.tsx`](../frontend/components/intelligence/attention.tsx) came to hold a screen that matches no design document.** ✅ **The design authority is named above and must be opened** ([D-138](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) |

### TASK-1426 · Session expiry and deep-link restoration — the intent survives the round trip

| | |
|---|---|
| ⚡ **Depends on clarification** ([D-208](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §3) | **[C-1410](LANE_COORDINATION.md) — rule 04's landing with no intent.** **Why it matters:** ⛔ **the row's whole subject is what survives an interruption, and rule 04 is the interruption.** **The assumption it would otherwise require:** 🔴 **a customer-visible destination for an expired session that was reaching for nothing** — **invented by this lane, shipped, and then contradicted whenever Design answers.** ✅ **Parallel work:** **everything else in workflow 29 proceeds** — [TASK-1402](#task-1402--the-intent-resolver--what-the-person-was-trying-to-reach) produces the intent this row later preserves, and it is unaffected |
| 🔴 **A gap the design does not cover, reported by Lane A and now owned** | 📁 **[C-250](LANE_COORDINATION.md) ④: *"Rule 04 `SESSION_EXPIRED`'s route column reads `(intent preserved)`. With no intent there is nothing to preserve, and the document does not say where that lands."*** ✅ **Lane A refused to invent it.** ⚡ **This row builds the mechanism and leaves the undefined case as the queued question** |
| **Epic** | **E1 · Entry Engine** — **[workflow 29](coordination/workflows.toml)** |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** |
| **Status** | ⏸️ **`BLOCKED` — Lane C, 2026-08-09, on [C-1410](LANE_COORDINATION.md).** ✅ **THE ONE ROW OF THE FOUR THAT IS GENUINELY DEPENDENT**, and [D-208](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §3's question is what proves it: ⛔ **rule 04's route reads `(intent preserved)` and the document does not say where that lands WHEN THERE IS NO INTENT.** 🔴 **The assumption this row would otherwise have to invent is a customer-visible destination** — **and inventing one is [§21](../CLAUDE.md)'s exact prohibition.** ⚠️ **Also blocked on the *Session Continuity* specification ([C-1405](LANE_COORDINATION.md)), which the Coverage Review calls *"the largest true gap in the audit"*.** *(Previously `SEQUENCED` behind [TASK-1402](#task-1402--the-intent-resolver--what-the-person-was-trying-to-reach), whose input it needs.)* |
| **Design authority** | ⛔ **`Fortune Signora Authenticated Entry.dc.html` §03 rules 04–09** |
| ⚡ **Delivers** | **A deep link followed while signed out survives the sign-in round trip and is honoured on return** — 📁 rule 06 `INTENT_HONOURED` · **an expired session preserves what the person was reaching for** · ⛔ **and the intent is DISCARDED when it is forbidden or names a workspace they do not belong to**, rather than retried |
| 🔴 **The security property** | ⛔ **A preserved intent is attacker-controllable input carried across an authentication boundary.** ✅ **It must be validated on return, never replayed** — **and `IntentAccess` is *"the server's answer, never this module's"*, which is the engine's own comment and the constraint this row honours** |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | ✅ **Better Auth's `callbackURL` already carries a destination across sign-in** — ⚡ **and [D-194](DECISION_REGISTER.md) found it RACING a manual `router.push`.** ⛔ **This row must not add a third mechanism: [TASK-1401](#task-1401--the-five-hardcoded-redirects-become-one-call-site) removes the manual push, and the intent rides the one that survives |
| **Reusable components required** | ✅ **None** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟠 **`frontend/lib/auth/**`** — announced |
| **Playwright coverage** | 🔴 **A signed-out person opens a deep link, signs in, and arrives at that link** · 🔴 **a deep link to a workspace they do not belong to does NOT arrive there and does not leak that it exists** |
| **Component coverage** | **An expired session preserves the intent; a forbidden intent is discarded with `INTENT_FORBIDDEN`** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | ✅ **n/a — no new surface beyond [TASK-1423](#task-1423--the-six-gate-screens--a-failure-that-is-designed-not-a-redirect-loop)'s** |
| 📱 **Responsive behaviour** | ✅ **n/a** |
| **Definition of Done** | **An intent survives sign-in, is validated on return, and is discarded rather than replayed when refused** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Security Invariant** 🔴 **an intent is never trusted across the boundary** — **the server re-answers `access` on return, and an open-redirect through a preserved intent is the failure this asserts against** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | 🔴 **Open redirect.** ⛔ **A preserved destination that is not validated against the person's memberships on RETURN is the classic form of it** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **BUY the transport — Better Auth's `callbackURL`. Build the validation** |

### TASK-1430 · Inbound rate limiting — nothing guards Signora's own API

| | |
|---|---|
| 🔴 **Why this exists** | ⛔ **Found by the [D-213](DECISION_REGISTER.md) production-readiness probe: `backend/apps/` has NO inbound rate limiting.** ⚠️ **38 files rate-limit OUTBOUND calls to vendors — HubSpot, AI providers, provider pools — and that is the opposite concern.** ⚡ **The first version of the probe matched those 38 and reported ✅**, which is the false assurance the checklist exists to prevent, produced by the checklist on its own first run |
| **Classification** ([D-208](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §4) | ⛔ **MISSING IMPLEMENTATION** — a platform capability, not a design question |
| **Epic** | **Platform Foundation** |
| **Module** | ✅ **`product_surface`** — **CORRECTED 2026-08-10 by [C-1420](LANE_COORDINATION.md), ruled Outcome B by the product owner.** ⛔ **This cell read `platform_foundation` and that was a CATEGORY ERROR, not an ownership question**: 📁 [`modules.toml`](coordination/modules.toml) defines `platform_foundation` as *"Environment framework, repository tooling, developer commands"*, and an ASGI middleware at the API edge is none of those. 📁 **[D-022](DECISION_REGISTER.md) enumerates exactly what Lane C builds inside the substrate — `signora_platform/simulation/**` and nothing else.** ⚡ **`signora_platform/` is NOT a module; it is frozen substrate governed by [§20a](../CLAUDE.md) exemption, which is why no module cleanly owns it and why the nearest-sounding name got written here.** ✅ **`product_surface` is where every authentication-surface row already lives** — [TASK-493](#task-493), [TASK-1410](#task-1410), [TASK-498](#task-498) — **and its context names *routing*.** ⛔ **The registry is UNCHANGED and was never wrong** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`READY` — Lane C, 2026-08-10.** ✅ **[ADR-0408](architecture/adr/0408-inbound-rate-limiting.md) is ACCEPTED ([D-232](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §4), and the [§20a](../CLAUDE.md) exemption is granted for exactly one ASGI middleware, its typed `Settings` and its Security Invariant** *(was `SEQUENCED` — needs the ADR before it is claimable)* |
| **Module** | **`platform_foundation`** — ⚠️ **implemented by LANE A, not by this office.** 📁 **[D-232](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §4: *do not implement the rate limiter in Lane C***. ⚡ **The §20a exemption is granted to the WORK, not to a lane** |
| ✅ **Implementation scope, explicit** | **① One ASGI middleware in `signora_platform/api/`, registered AFTER correlation-id assignment so a refused request stays traceable** ([§16](../CLAUDE.md)) · **② a typed `Settings` owned by the platform, injected at the composition root** ([§6](../CLAUDE.md)) · **③ a Security Invariant.** ⛔ **Nothing else in the platform changes** |
| ✅ **Routes covered — authentication abuse, NOT general throttling** | **`/signin` · `/register` · password-reset REQUEST · verification RESEND.** 🔴 **Reset-request and resend are not lesser cases — they make Signora SEND MAIL on an attacker's command**, which needs no valid credential. ⚠️ **SSO initiation: assess, do not assume.** ⛔ **Authenticated-route throttling is a DIFFERENT policy and is out of scope** ([ADR-0408](architecture/adr/0408-inbound-rate-limiting.md) §13) |
| ✅ **Reusable mechanism — Lane A's [L-1](../ENGINEERING_LAWS.md) audit, not this office's guess** | ⛔ **This office deliberately names NO library.** ⚡ **What it names is the requirement: a SHARED counter, because an in-process one gives an attacker *n* × the budget across *n* replicas and loosens silently as the service scales.** 📁 **Redis is already a backing service (ARQ, the outbox relay) attached by URL — no new datastore** |
| 🔴 **Failure behaviour — RULED, and it is the part that is easy to get wrong** | ✅ **FAIL-OPEN, and LOUD.** ⛔ **Fail-closed turns a Redis outage into a sign-in outage for every customer, which is the larger harm.** ⚠️ **But fail-open is only acceptable BECAUSE it is observable** — **an explicit short timeout owned by the module, allow-on-error, a failure counter, a WARN carrying `correlation_id`, and an ALERTABLE degraded state.** 📁 **A silent fail-open is [§2](../CLAUDE.md)'s decorative guard** |
| 🔴 **The key strategy, and it is a Security Invariant not a detail** | ⛔ **Key on what an attacker cannot choose on somebody else's behalf.** **Keying sign-in solely on the submitted address lets anybody lock any customer out by spending their budget — the control becomes the attack.** ✅ **Assert: a refused caller can never cause the refusal of a caller they do not control** |
| ⛔ **Numeric thresholds — NOT decided, and not Lane A's to invent either** | **Every threshold is configuration in the module's typed `Settings`, changed without a code change.** 📁 **[ADR-0408](architecture/adr/0408-inbound-rate-limiting.md) §15: no production number is derivable — there is no traffic to measure and no security requirement specifying one.** ✅ **A conservative default ships; tuning follows measurement** |
| ✅ **Required tests** | **Unit** — the limiter refuses at the boundary and allows below it · **Security Invariant** — ⛔ **a refused caller cannot cause the refusal of another** · ⛔ **a `429` is byte-identical whether or not the address exists** (the enumeration rule the sign-in copy already follows) · **Failure-mode** — 🔴 **Redis unavailable → the request is ALLOWED, the counter increments and a WARN is emitted; Redis SLOW → the timeout bounds it and sign-in latency does not grow unbounded.** ⚡ **Both failure tests are the point of the slice, not extras** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟠 **`signora_platform/api/` — one middleware and its `Settings`, under the [D-232](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §4 exemption, announced in the commit** · 🟢 **`backend/tests/security_invariants/`** · 🔴 **any other platform internal** |
| 🔴 **Why it matters now rather than later** | **Four authentication journeys are on `main`**: sign-in, registration, verification and password reset. ⛔ **`/sign-in` accepts unlimited attempts from one address**, and [C-431](LANE_COORDINATION.md) already records that **no lockout capability exists** — 📁 which is why `workflows.toml` EXCLUDES screens *11 Account locked* and *12 Too many attempts* from Workflow 1. ⚡ **Those two screens are designed, and this is the capability that makes them reachable** |
| ✅ **Delivers** | **A limiter at the edge**, applied per route rather than globally · **the authentication routes first** · ⛔ **and a response that does not disclose whether the address exists** — **the same account-enumeration rule the sign-in card's copy already follows** |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | 📁 **`signora_platform/observability/` already carries correlation context and metrics**, so a refusal is countable and traceable without new plumbing · ⚠️ **the store is the open question**: in-process is useless behind more than one worker |
| **Change Budget** | 🔴 **`signora_platform/`** — **ADR required** |
| **Definition of Done** | **A route can declare a limit, a refusal is recorded, and screens `11` and `12` become reachable** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Security Invariant** 🔴 **a refusal must not reveal whether the account exists** · **the limit is enforced server-side and per identity as well as per address** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | ⛔ **An in-process counter.** ✅ **It passes every test on one worker and protects nothing in production** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ⚠️ **Commodity — evaluate `slowapi` or a gateway rule against [09-build-vs-buy.md](architecture/09-build-vs-buy.md) before writing one** |

### TASK-1431 · A performance budget, and something that notices when it moves

| | |
|---|---|
| 🔴 **Why this exists** | ⛔ **Found by the [D-213](DECISION_REGISTER.md) probe: 21 Playwright specs, one accessibility spec, and NO performance measurement of any kind.** ⚡ **Nothing in the repository would notice a regression** — not a slower query, not a heavier bundle, not a slower first paint |
| **Classification** ([D-208](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §4) | ⛔ **MISSING IMPLEMENTATION** |
| **Epic** | **Platform Foundation** |
| **Module** | **`platform_foundation`** |
| **Status** | 🔵 **`CLAIMED` — Lane C, 2026-08-10. ✅ UNBLOCKED and the baseline is MEASURED.** 🔴 **[B-219](BUGS.md) never blocked this row and Lane A proved it rather than arguing**: `npx next build` with no dev server running succeeds and emits the full per-route report. ⛔ **B-219 damages only a dev server running AT THE SAME TIME — which its own title says — so this was a sequencing note I read as a blocker, and I held a row for it.** ⚠️ **I then reported *"no claimable work exists"* while the work was runnable.** ✅ **MEASURED 2026-08-10 on this lane's clone, `next build`, no dev server: shared by all **102 kB** · authenticated entry `/w/[workspace]/home` **159 kB** First Load — **the largest route in the tree** · next `/settings/team` **156 kB** · `/settings/products` 148 kB · `/signin` 143 kB.** ⛔ **No budget is set in this cell** — 📁 the row requires *what ships today plus a stated margin*, and the margin is the next commit, not this one |
| ⚡ **Delivers** | **A budget, and one measurement against it.** ⛔ **Not a performance programme** — **a number that fails a build when it moves the wrong way** |
| 🔴 **What it must NOT do** | ⛔ **Ship red.** 📁 **[D-162](DECISION_REGISTER.md)'s lesson, in this office's own words: *a gate that is red on arrival is a broken gate*.** ✅ **Measure first, set the budget at what ships today plus a stated margin, then enforce** |
| ✅ **Scope, deliberately small** | **Bundle size for the authenticated entry path**, and **the `(app)` shell's first paint.** ⛔ **Not every route, not a lighthouse score, not a synthetic-monitoring stack** — ⚡ **the shell is what every workflow inherits, so a regression there is a regression everywhere** |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | 📁 **`observability/metrics.py` exists** — server-side timing needs no new plumbing · **the Playwright harness runs locally, through `make e2e`** ⚠️ **CORRECTED 2026-08-10 — this cell read *"the Playwright harness already runs in CI"* and that is false.** 📁 **Measured: `.github/workflows/` holds `backend.yml`, `frontend.yml` and `smoke.yml`, and all three are `workflow_dispatch:` only** ([§20c-2](../CLAUDE.md)) — ⛔ **nothing runs on a push, so a budget wired to CI would never execute** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`frontend/tests/`** · 🟢 **the local gate it runs under** — ⛔ **NOT the CI workflow.** ⚠️ **CORRECTED 2026-08-10: this cell read 🟠 *"the CI workflow — announced"*, which would have walked a lane into a ⚫ BLACK change at amber authority.** 📁 **[§20c-2](../CLAUDE.md) is absolute — *"Claude must never create or re-enable: GitHub Actions…"* — and adding a `push:` trigger to a retained workflow is prohibited by name.** ✅ **Under [ADR-0027](architecture/adr/0027-local-first-development.md) the local run is the only run, so the budget belongs in a local gate; that is not a weaker home for it, it is the only one** |
| **Definition of Done** | **A budget exists, a run measures it, and exceeding it fails** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `platform_foundation` · **Playwright** one budget spec |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | ⛔ **A flaky budget teaches people to retry the build.** ✅ **Pick a metric that is deterministic — bundle bytes before first-paint milliseconds** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ⚠️ **Commodity — the bundler already reports size; start there** |

| | |
|---|---|
| 🔴 **Classification** | ⛔ **INCORRECT IMPLEMENTATION — rebuild, do not enhance** ([D-217](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §5) |

| **Module** | **`product_surface`** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE**, 2026-08-10, on `main`. **Previously:** ✅ **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-10.** ✅ **Everything [D-225](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §1 released**: hierarchy, levels, labels, icons, active state, count slot, pinned utility group, structure, responsive and collapse — **all the design's now, none of it before.** 🔴 **§4 decided the ENTRY SET and `/` and `/business` survive**, though the design names neither: **nine of its thirteen entries have no route**, so a literal rebuild removes two working destinations and adds nine that 404. ⚡ **A restricted destination is now VISIBLE at 45% with a lock and explains on click, naming who can grant it** — 📁 *"a hidden destination teaches a person the feature does not exist, so they ask for the wrong thing or ask nobody."* ⛔ **That REVERSES this component's own rule**, and [D-154](DECISION_REGISTER.md) settles it: *the design wins, always.* 🔴 **Reachability is unchanged** — no link, and Casbin still refuses ([§20c](../CLAUDE.md)). 🔴 **Six of `lib/shell/rail.ts`'s exports had ZERO production callers** — `MAX_DESTINATIONS`, `MAX_DEPTH`, `TAB_ORDER`, `RailDestination`, `railViolations`, `visibleDestinations` — **the rail's law written, tested and never consulted by the rail.** ✅ **This row consumes it, and `railViolations` now runs at module load in development.** ⚠️ **A test passed straight through the reversal and was rewritten, not deleted**: it queried for a *link* named Settings, and the restricted rendering is a `span` — **so it kept returning null and kept passing while its subject inverted.** ⚡ **The member journey was updated for the same reason and now asserts the explanation.** ✅ **Fire-tested**: restoring the hide makes two tests fail. **2224 vitest / 110 files (+11, +1) · tsc 0 · eslint 0.** **Was: `CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-10** |
| ✅ **What this row builds, and all of it is specified** ([D-225](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §1) | **Hierarchy · levels · labels · icons · active state · the count slot · the pinned utility group · navigation structure · responsive and collapse behaviour · shell integration.** 📁 **From [`Fortune Signora App Shell.dc.html`](design-snapshot/screens/), which is the authority** — ⛔ **and the existing implementation is a *candidate*, never the authority** (§3). **If it does not match, scrap and rebuild; do not decorate an incorrect implementation until it resembles the design** |
| 🔴 **The retention rule — RULED, not recommended** ([D-225](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §4) | ⛔ ***"Do not remove currently valid, reachable navigation that is required for an existing user role."*** ⚡ **Concretely: `/` and `/business` stay until their design-named successors exist.** 📁 **`GUARDED_ROUTES` guards `/settings` alone, so a `member` sees those two and nothing else; removing them leaves a navigation of one — the defect CX-05 fixed on 2026-08-03, and [C-008](LANE_COORDINATION.md) records `/business` as a member's only door to five declarations `policy.csv` grants them.** ⛔ ***"The unresolved design question must not create a functional regression"*** |
| ⛔ **And still settled — an entry that leads nowhere is not rendered** | 📁 **[C-248](LANE_COORDINATION.md), and `navigation.tsx`'s own D38 comment agree: not disabled, not *coming soon*, not hidden-but-present.** ⚡ **So the nine design entries with no route are simply absent until they have one; the `Accounts` entry's destination is [TASK-440](#task-440--companies--the-account-list-a-seller-actually-works), `BLOCKED` behind [TASK-472](#task-472--a-workspace-can-list-its-accounts-and-blank-input-currently-refuses-to)** |
| ✅ **Lane C's answer — verified, not accepted** | 📁 **Lane A's measurement is correct in every particular and I re-ran it rather than trusting it.** ⚡ **`find frontend/app -name page.tsx`: `/accounts` has NO index, only `/accounts/[accountId]`; `/` and `/business` exist; `/signals` and `/settings` exist; the other nine have no route.** 🔴 **AND ONE FACT NEITHER OF US HAD: the design carries no `href` AT ALL.** 📁 **`navItems` entries are `[label, icon, count, level, active]`** — ⛔ **so the design specifies STRUCTURE, never destinations, and *"rebuild to the design"* is fully satisfiable on hierarchy, states, the pinned utility group and the count slot without deciding the entry set.** ✅ **That half is unblocked and needs no ruling** |
| 🔴 **The one question, and it is a capability regression rather than a visual one** | ⛔ **`GUARDED_ROUTES` guards `/settings` and nothing else, so a `member` sees `/` and `/business` — and NOTHING ELSE — today.** 🔴 **Render only the design's entries that resolve and a member sees `Signals` alone: `Settings` is guarded and hidden from them.** 📁 **A navigation of ONE is the exact defect CX-05 measured on 2026-08-03 and fixed by adding `/business`, in `navigation.tsx`'s own words: *"a `member` saw a sidebar with one item in it… A navigation of one is not navigation; it is a label."*** ⛔ **And `/business` is *"the only door"* — [C-008](LANE_COORDINATION.md): for a member there is no other route to five declarations `policy.csv` grants them.** ⚡ **So the question is not *does the rail match the design*; it is: **do `/` and `/business` leave the rail before their design-named successors exist?** 🔴 **That removes working, member-reachable navigation and re-creates a defect a product review fixed seven days ago, so it is the product owner's and this office will not take it** |
| ⚡ **Lane C's recommendation** ([§20e](../CLAUDE.md) — a recommendation, never a decision) | ✅ **Adopt the design's STRUCTURE now; RETAIN the two working entries until their design-named successors exist.** **Each new screen then adds its design row and retires the interim one, so the rail converges on the design without a day where a salesperson has a label instead of a navigation.** ⛔ **Lane A's option ② is already settled and this office does not re-open it: do not render an entry to a route that does not exist — not disabled, not *coming soon*, not hidden-but-present** ([C-248](LANE_COORDINATION.md), and the D38 comment agrees) |
| 🔴 **The measurement** | 📁 **The design draws EIGHT primary entries and two sub-entries**: **`Home · Forecast · Pipeline · Accounts (Enterprise, Mid-market) · Deals · Conversations · Signals · Reports`**, ⚡ **plus three utilities pinned to the bottom: `Settings · Shortcuts · Docs & support`.** ⛔ **`navigation.tsx:68–70` ships THREE: `Attention · Your business · Settings`.** 🔴 **Not one of the three appears in the design, and not one of the design's eleven appears in the product** |
| 🔴 **Why this survived, and it is a governance failure rather than a coding one** | 📁 **The file's own comment says where it came from**: *"Two entries, reviewed from first principles at M12.3 (D38)"*, then *"Three entries from CX-05, and the third had to earn it."* ⚡ **Every word of that reasoning is sound and it is reasoning from PRODUCT REVIEW, dated before [D-128](DECISION_REGISTER.md) made the 26 finalized designs canonical** — 📁 ***"existing code is not the authority. Historical UI is not the authority."*** ⛔ **D-128 did not reach this file because nothing re-derived it, and [C-306](LANE_COORDINATION.md) — which is exactly this conflict — has sat `OPEN` marked *becomes P1 the moment a refinement slice reaches that screen*.** 🔴 **It reached it. Nobody noticed** |

Nothing was built on the wrong count; it is corrected before anything is** — 📁 *"Hover: sunken tint. Active: brand tint, 600 label, brand icon. Focus: 1px brand border plus a 3px tint halo — never the browser ring."* · **second level indents to 30px and never nests further** · **restricted destinations stay visible at 45% with a lock and explain on click** · **counts are neutral; a dot means unread and only Signals uses it** |

| 🔴 **Two of the shipped controls are in the top bar against a rule the shell already enforces** | 📁 **Shell rule 02: *"No module may add anything to the top bar."*** ⛔ **The theme toggle and the sign-out button are in it, and `lib/shell/constitution.ts` exports that rule as executable code while the frame beside it breaks it** |

the token is the value for both.** ⚠️ **Two of this row's own tests were hollow and were repaired before shipping**: one asserted **nothing at all**, and one used `.click()` so it may have checked a closed menu. ✅ **Both fire-tested after repair.** ✅ **And the top-bar test written to be loud on this day was loud.** **2258 vitest / 112 files (+13, +1) · tsc 0 · eslint 0.** **Was: `CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-10** |
| ⚡ **How this row reached Lane A** *(Lane C, 2026-08-10)* | ✅ **This office released it to `READY` minutes before the claim, on measured evidence: `navigation.tsx` on the lane branch carried `Forecast`, `Pipeline`, `Conversations`, `Reports`, so the rail was rebuilt.** ⛔ **`CLAIMED` is the lane's and [§20e](../CLAUDE.md) reserves it — this office released the row, it did not claim it.** ⚠️ **Recorded HERE rather than appended to the status line above, which is [B-417](BUGS.md): appending to a line resolved in another lane's favour breaks that lane's next commit, and it did** |

### TASK-1443 · The shell's four breakpoints are the design's, not Tailwind's defaults

| | |
|---|---|
| 🔴 **Classification** | ⛔ **INCORRECT IMPLEMENTATION** |
| **Design authority** | 📁 **App Shell `breakpoints`** |
| **Epic** | **Common Platform Foundation** — **[workflow 13](coordination/workflows.toml)** |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE**, 2026-08-10, in certified `f22b89d`. **Previously:** ✅ **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-10.** ✅ **The design's four bands REPLACE Tailwind's defaults** — 📁 `screens` overrides rather than extends, **deliberately**: leaving `md:`/`sm:` beside them would give the frame two vocabularies for one question. **Named for the band, not the number**, so a boundary moving does not scatter magic widths. 🔴 **The frame is rebuilt on them**: the rail's band was **`md:` = 768**, so **the rail vanished 256px earlier than the design says and the phone bar appeared on a small laptop that should have had the collapsed rail.** 📁 The design's boundary is *"< 1024px: rail becomes a sheet."* 🔴 **And `deviceClassFor` hardcoded `768` INSIDE the rail's own law** — ⛔ a number the App Shell never names. ✅ **`BREAKPOINTS` and `bandFor` are the design's four**, and ⚡ **a test parses the widths out of the design document**, so a refresh that moves a boundary fails rather than passing over a frame that no longer matches. ⚠️ **A test's NAME claimed the design's breakpoint while its BODY asserted Tailwind's `768`** — ⛔ **worse than no test, because it read as coverage of the rule it named.** ✅ **Corrected.** ✅ **[C-278](LANE_COORDINATION.md) CLOSED, and by this lane rather than by the product owner** — 📁 the App Shell says *"the rail spans the full height beneath the banners"*, **which is this shell exactly**, so `header[banner]` describes DOM and tab order rather than one element. ⛔ **The restructure this lane nearly proposed would have CONTRADICTED the design.** **2474 vitest / 122 files (+2) · tsc 0 · eslint 0.** **Was: `CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-10** |
| 🔴 **The measurement** | 📁 **The design specifies four: `≥1600` · `1280–1599` (the reference layout) · `1024–1279` · `<1024`.** ⛔ **`app-shell.tsx` uses `md:` and `sm:` only — Tailwind's 768px and 640px.** ⚡ **So every responsive decision in the frame fires at a width the design never named**, and the 1024–1279 band — 📁 *"rail collapses to 56px icons with tooltips, utility panel closes to a 28px tab rail, filter strip stays"* — **does not exist at all** |
| ⚡ **Delivers** | **Four named breakpoints as tokens, and the frame's behaviour at each** — ⛔ **not a media-query sweep of every component** |
| **Definition of Done** | **The frame behaves as the design's four bands describe, at the design's widths** · **certified · `main`** |
| 📱 **Responsive behaviour** | 📁 **`<1024`: rail becomes a sheet from a top-bar button; filter strip collapses to one `Scope` button with a count; tables become row cards. `<640`: review, approve, comment and ask only** |
| ⚠️ **Risk** | ⛔ **Redefining Tailwind's global breakpoints and moving every screen at once.** ✅ **Name the four, apply them in the SHELL; screens migrate on their own rows** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **n/a** |

| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE**, 2026-08-10, in certified `f22b89d`. **Previously:** ✅ **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-10.** ✅ **Skip links (2.4.1) — there were ZERO; now two, `sr-only focus:not-sr-only` so they are FOCUSABLE while invisible**, which is the whole mechanism. ⛔ **The utility-panel link is ABSENT because the panel is [TASK-1433](#task-1433--the-utility-panel-behind-the-content-its-three-tabs-name)'s and does not exist** — a skip link to a missing landmark sends a keyboard user nowhere. ✅ **Landmarks**: the rail is **`Primary`**, not `Main` — 📁 the design's word, **and `Main` collided with the `main` landmark beside it** · **a `search` landmark labelled *Search Fortune Signora***, which the slot published none of · **`main` is `tabIndex={-1}`** so the skip link lands. ✅ **Route announcement (4.1.3)** — polite, and **silent on first mount because arriving is not a route CHANGE.** ⚠️ **TWO ERRORS OF MINE, both caught by the tests and both recorded**: ① **wrapping the banner strip in a `<header>` gave the shell TWO `banner` landmarks** — ⛔ **worse than the gap**, since a screen-reader user choosing *banner* would get a menu of two; **reverted, and a test now asserts exactly one.** 🔴 **The design's single header holding banners AND the top bar cannot be expressed while the rail is full-height — [C-278](LANE_COORDINATION.md).** ② **the 2.4.7 check was too blunt and flagged three files, none a defect** — two used `focus-within:` and the third suppresses the ring on a `tabIndex={-1}` panel it focuses in code. ✅ **The rule now distinguishes a TAB STOP from a programmatically-focused container**, which is what 2.4.7 actually protects. ✅ **Fire-tested**: hiding the skip links and reverting the rail's label each fail. **2472 vitest / 122 files (+21, +1) · tsc 0 · eslint 0.** **Was: `CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-10** |
| 🔴 **The measurement** | ⛔ **Skip links: the design requires THREE — to content, to navigation, to the utility panel, visible on focus. `grep` finds NONE.** ⛔ **`nav` is labelled `"Main"`; the design says `"Primary"`.** ⛔ **No `search` landmark.** ⛔ **Nothing announces a route change politely (4.1.3)** |
| ⚡ **Delivers** | **The three skip links · the design's landmark names · the 2px brand focus ring at 2px offset, never suppressed · a polite route-change announcement · reflow to 320px at 400% zoom** |
