### TASK-1447 · The loading and error states every route owes

| | |
|---|---|
| 🔴 **Classification** | ⛔ **MISSING — [P-A](DOMAIN_DISCOVERY_REGISTER.md) at platform scale** |
| **Design authority** | 📁 **[Design System v2](design-snapshot/screens/) §13 *States*** · 📁 **[System States](design-snapshot/screens/)** |
| **Epic** | **Common Platform Foundation** — **[workflow 13](coordination/workflows.toml)** |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE**, 2026-08-10, in certified `9b4082e`, Closeout `964131d`. **Previously `IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-10:** ✅ **`loading.tsx` on every route that reads — 0 of 26 became 12 of 12** · ✅ **an error path on the read that had none.** 🔴 **THE SHARPEST INSTANCE WAS THIS LANE'S OWN, SHIPPED TWO ROWS AGO**: 📁 `/help` had no error branch, so **a failed `useIntegrations` rendered as *"none are connected yet"*** — ⛔ **[P-A](DOMAIN_DISCOVERY_REGISTER.md) exactly: a failed read rendering as an empty result**, telling a customer their workspace is empty when Signora could not look. ✅ **Fixed with `ErrorState`, a correlation id and a retry.** ⚡ **Found by measuring for this row rather than by review.** ✅ **REACH, not new components** — `SkeletonText`, `ErrorState` reused; ⛔ **no second vocabulary.** ⚠️ **TWO MEASUREMENT ERRORS OF MY OWN, both caught and both recorded in the guard**: ① the first scan read only `page.tsx`, and `/signals` and `/profile` delegate to components — **it measured the wrong file**; ② the *reads data* heuristic matched `use[A-Z]\w*(`, so **`useState` made every auth form look like a read.** ✅ **An IMPORT from the query layer is the honest signal**, and the guard says so. 🔴 **And the guard immediately earned itself**: it caught `/join/[workspaceId]`, **which my manual sweep had missed** — 📁 the sweep lists routes a person thought of; the guard lists routes that actually read. **2382 vitest / 116 files (+84, +2) · tsc 0 · eslint 0.** **Was: `CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-10** |
| 🔴 **The measurement, per route directory** | ⛔ **`loading.tsx`: 0 of 26.** ⛔ **`ErrorState` or `SystemState` rendered: 2 of 26.** ⚠️ **`EmptyState` rendered: 7 of 26.** ⚡ **Three and a half times more screens have a designed EMPTY state than a designed ERROR state** |
| 🔴 **Why the ratio is the defect and not the counts** | 📁 **[P-A](DOMAIN_DISCOVERY_REGISTER.md): *a failed read must never render as an empty result*.** ⚡ **[C-226](LANE_COORDINATION.md) found this six times on ONE screen and it was fixed there.** ⛔ **The measurement above is the same defect across the product** — **on most screens a failed read is indistinguishable from no data**, and the customer is told there is nothing rather than that we could not find out |
| ✅ **What already exists and must be reused** | 📁 **`components/ui/system-state.tsx` ✅ 4 importers · `error-state.tsx` ✅ 1 · `empty-state.tsx` ✅ 19 · `skeleton.tsx` ✅ 16 · `region-boundary.tsx`** — ⛔ **do not build a second vocabulary; this row is about REACH, not new components** |
| ⚡ **Delivers** | **A `loading.tsx` for every route that reads**, composing `Skeleton` · **an error path on every read**, composing `ErrorState`/`SystemState` · ⛔ **and the two are never the same rendering** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`app/**/loading.tsx`** · 🟢 **the read sites in each route** |
| **Playwright coverage** | 🔴 **The one that matters: a read that FAILS renders an error with a retry, never an empty state** |
| **Component coverage** | **A failing query and an empty query render different components** — ⛔ **the assertion fails if they render the same** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | 📁 **A loading region is `aria-busy` and announces politely; an error is `role="alert"`** |
| **Definition of Done** | **Every route that reads has a loading state and a distinct error state, and a test proves the two differ** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Adoption is this row's, not each workflow's** ([D-219](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §5) | 📁 ***"Then identify existing screens that need to adopt it. Do not wait for each workflow to rediscover the problem."*** ⚡ **The 26-route measurement in [498 §4.2](architecture/498-common-platform-audit.md) IS that list** — ⛔ **so the sweep is already done and the row inherits it; what remains is doing the work rather than finding it again** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `product_surface` · **Playwright** one forced-failure journey |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | ⛔ **Adding `loading.tsx` files that render nothing, to make the count 26.** ✅ **A skeleton that does not match the shape it replaces is worse than a spinner** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **n/a** |

| 🔴 **The measurement** | ⛔ **No `tabs`, `avatar` or `tooltip` in [`components/ui`](../frontend/components/ui).** 🔴 **`app/(app)/signals/signal-queue.tsx` implements `role="tab"` locally** — ⚡ **the ONLY workflow-local reimplementation the [498](architecture/498-common-platform-audit.md) reuse sweep found, and it is how the duplication problem starts** |
| ⚡ **Why the three travel together** | **Each is a dependency of a row already `READY`**: 📁 **Tabs → Account 360 and the utility panel · Avatar → [TASK-1440](BACKLOG.md)'s fifth top-bar slot · Tooltip → [TASK-1444](BACKLOG.md)'s collapsed-rail requirement (1.4.13: hoverable, dismissible, persistent)** |

| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE**, 2026-08-10, in certified `f22b89d`. **Previously:** ✅ **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-10.** ✅ **Moved into `components/ui` with `git mv`, all eight importers repointed, and nothing left at the old path** — ⚡ **a move that leaves the original is not a move, it is a second copy.** ✅ **The design's three kinds**: `success` · `undo` · `failure`, each with its action (`Download` · `Undo` · `View`). 🔴 **THE THIRD IS THE ONE THAT MUST NOT AUTO-DISMISS, and the design writes the rule into the copy**: *"8,400 synced. **This stays until dismissed**."* ⛔ **Auto-dismissing a failure the person has not read is how a failed action becomes an unexplained one** — and it is the failure that carries the correlation id. 🔴 **It closes [TASK-1452](#task-1452--scrap-and-rebuild--the-table-is-the-v1-table-design-system-v2-was-written-to-replace)'s open half**: that row's inline editing must *"surface undo in the toast for 10 seconds"* and **this component had no action and a 6000ms dismissal**, so `EditableCell` handed both values back and **the undo had nowhere to go.** ✅ **`UNDO_DISMISS_MS` is the design's number.** ⚡ **No caller was rewritten**: `error` aliases to `failure` — ✅ measured, **every `toast()` call in the product passes `success` or `error`, and `info` had a type and no caller.** ⚠️ **A test that PINNED the misplacement was re-aimed, not deleted** — 📁 its own words were *"relocating a live component is a change to every caller, not a design-system slice"*, **and this row is that slice.** ✅ **Fire-tested both dismissal rules.** **2417 vitest / 118 files (+8, +1) · tsc 0 · eslint 0.** **Was: `CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-10** |
| 🔴 **The measurement** | ⛔ **`components/feedback/toast.tsx` — a shared platform primitive living outside `components/ui`.** 📁 **[§20c](../CLAUDE.md): *"a genuinely new component belongs in `components/ui` with its tokens and its test"*** |
| 📁 **The three kinds the design draws** | **Success with an action** — *"Export ready — 8,412 rows"* / `Download` · **Optimistic commit with undo** — *"Close date updated · committed optimistically"* / `Undo`, ⚡ **which §11 of the table spec says persists 10 seconds** · **Partial failure that STAYS UNTIL DISMISSED** — *"Sync failed for 12 accounts. 8,400 synced."* |
| ⚡ **Delivers** | **The move into `components/ui`, the three kinds, and the dismissal rule** — ⛔ **the third kind is the one that is usually missed, and it is the one that must not auto-dismiss** |

| **Change Budget** | 🟠 **A component moves into `components/ui`** — announce it; **update every importer in the same change** |
| **Component coverage** | **A failure toast does not auto-dismiss** · **an undo toast survives 10 seconds** · **rule 11 is asserted: a toast carrying required reading fails** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | 📁 **`role="status"` for confirmations, `role="alert"` for the failure kind** |

### TASK-1450 · Charts, and the eight-series ramp that does not exist

| | |
|---|---|
| 🔴 **Classification** | ⛔ **MISSING — component and tokens both** |
| **Design authority** | 📁 **[Design System v2](design-snapshot/screens/) `chartRamp` — eight series, `--c1`…`--c8`, with a separate dark ramp** |
| **Epic** | **Common Platform Foundation** — **[workflow 13](coordination/workflows.toml)** |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE**, 2026-08-10, in certified `f22b89d`. **Previously:** ✅ **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-10.** ✅ **`--c1`…`--c8` in BOTH themes** — 📁 **the design gives a SEPARATE dark ramp rather than a filter**, and ⚡ **a test asserts the two DIFFER**, because a dark block that merely repeated the light values would pass a presence check and be unreadable on dark. ✅ **Fire-tested.** 🔴 **A series is a POSITION, not a colour a caller picks** — ⛔ **there is no `color` prop and the test asserts its absence.** 📁 The ramp opens on the brand and moves away from it, so a two-series chart reads as *ours versus theirs*; **a caller that could choose would eventually pick `--c5` first and the meaning would be gone with nothing to detect it.** ✅ **Two primitives, not a library** — 📁 the row is explicit: *"not every chart type; the ones those two screens draw."* ⛔ **A charting library ahead of its callers generalises the wrong axis** ([§6](../CLAUDE.md)), **and this repository has found a component with no caller six times.** ♿ **Each chart states its data**, because **a screen reader meets an SVG as a blank rectangle** — the bar series renders a list and hides the bars so no figure is read twice. ⚡ **Edge cases the data actually has**: one point renders nothing (a flat line would assert a direction), and a flat series does not divide by zero. **2430 vitest / 119 files (+13, +1) · tsc 0 · eslint 0.** **Was: `CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-10** |
| 🔴 **The measurement** | ⛔ **`--c1` through `--c8` are absent from `app/globals.css`** — ⚡ **the ONE part of the token layer that does not match, against a design system [D-217](DECISION_REGISTER.md) otherwise measured as exact.** ⛔ **And no chart component exists anywhere in `components/`** |
| ⚡ **Why the tokens come first** | 📁 **The design gives a light ramp AND a dark ramp.** ⛔ **A chart built before the tokens will hardcode eight colours**, which is the [§20c](../CLAUDE.md) violation the token test already forbids for every other colour — ⚡ **and `design-tokens.test.tsx` cannot catch it, because there is no token to compare against** |
| ⚡ **Delivers** | **The eight-series ramp as tokens in both themes**, then **the chart primitives the Executive Dashboard and Reports need** — ⛔ **not every chart type; the ones those two screens draw** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟠 **New tokens · a new component family in `components/ui`** — announce both |
| **Component coverage** | **Series colour comes from a token in both themes** — ⛔ **a hex literal in a chart fails the design-token test** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | 📁 **Series are distinguishable without colour** — **a legend and a non-colour encoding, per §17** |
| **Definition of Done** | **The ramp exists in both themes and one real screen renders a chart from it** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `product_surface` |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | ⛔ **Adopting a charting library inside this row.** 🔴 **That is a new dependency — [§6](../CLAUDE.md), product owner and an ADR.** ✅ **If one is wanted, this row stops and raises it** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | 🔴 **Genuinely open, and NOT decided here** — **charting is a commodity and the repository hand-rolls its UI.** ⚡ **The tokens are needed either way and are this row's independent half** |

| **Module** | **`product_surface`** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE**, 2026-08-10, in certified `9b4082e`, Closeout `964131d`. **Previously `IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-10:** 🔴 **Scrapped and rebuilt, not enhanced** — 📁 `border-collapse` with a per-row `border-b` **was** §11's 2px cage, so the structure encoded the old design and had to go. ✅ **All four rules**: **① pinned identity** as a 24px `::after` gradient with ⛔ **no `border-r`** — the seam the rule exists to remove · **② hover-and-selection with a 2px brand EDGE, not only a tint** — 📁 *distinguishable without colour vision*, and `aria-selected` because neither edge nor tint reaches a screen reader · **③ inline editing** — 6px focus ring, ⏎ commits, Escape reverts, and **both values handed back so undo has something to restore** · **④ the AI summary row, which is NOT a `<tr>`** — 📁 *never counted as a data row* is **unrepresentable rather than avoided.** ⚡ **Plus the 16px plane the old component had no version of.** ⚠️ **THE ROW SAID TWELVE CALLERS. MEASURED: two files, three tables, 33 element usages** — ✅ **reported rather than accepted**, and **all three are migrated in this change** with their identity column pinned. 🔴 **A real bug I introduced and caught by MEASURING, not reasoning**: a trailing `relative` on `TH` — `cn` is tailwind-merge, `relative` and `sticky` are the same property, **and the pin silently disappeared.** ⚡ **Found by printing the rendered class list.** ⚠️ **`table.tsx` joins the AI-hue allowlist on the guard's own criterion** — 📁 §14 reserves the pairing for *whether a machine wrote what you are reading*, and §11 rule ④ assigns it here. ⛔ **Only the summary row uses it.** ⚠️ **`EditableCell` has NO CALLER YET** — the editable table §11 describes (*close dates and amounts*) does not exist; **stated plainly rather than left to become the seventh instance of the law-without-a-surface pattern.** **2292 vitest / 114 files (+14, +1) · tsc 0 · eslint 0.** **Was: `CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-10** |
| 🔴 **The measurement — the whole component** | ⛔ **`components/ui/table.tsx` is six exports over `<table className="w-full border-collapse text-sm">`, a `<thead>` with `border-b`, and a `<tr>` with `border-b`.** ⚡ **Nothing else** |
| 🔴 **The four rules, none of which exists** | ⛔ **Pinned identity** — 📁 *"the first column pins with a 24px gradient fade instead of a border, so horizontal scroll never produces a hard seam"* · ⛔ **Hover and selection** — 📁 *"hover is the sunken tint. Selection adds a 2px brand edge and a brand-tint fill — distinguishable without colour vision"* · ⛔ **Inline editing** — 📁 *"editable cells reveal a 6px ring on focus, commit on ⏎ optimistically, and surface undo in the toast for 10 seconds"* · ⛔ **AI summary row** — 📁 *"pinned above the data it describes, tinted with the intelligence hue, dismissible, and never counted as a data row"* |
| ⛔ **And the plane** | 📁 **§05 assigns the table plane `--r-xl`, 16px.** ⛔ **`table.tsx` has no radius and no plane at all** — 📁 §11's critique names exactly this: *"Toolbars, footers and headers all carried 2px rules and full-width fills, so the data competed with its own container"* |

| 🔴 **The one that is a product capability, not styling** | 📁 ***"In practice reps edit close dates and amounts dozens of times a day"*** — ⛔ **inline editing is absent, so every correction opens a drawer, which is the workflow §11 exists to remove** |

| **Component coverage** | **Selection is distinguishable without colour** — ⛔ **an assertion that reads only the fill fails** · **the AI summary row is not counted in the row count** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | 📁 **1.4.10 reflow and 2.5.8 target size** · ⛔ **an editable cell is reachable and committable by keyboard alone** |

### TASK-1453 · The type scale is not the design's, and three surfaces apply the radius scale wrongly

| | |
|---|---|
| 🔴 **Classification** ([D-220](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §3) | ⛔ **C for the type scale — two roles absent, one wrong.** ⚠️ **B for the three applications** |
| **Design authority** | 📁 **[Design System v2](design-snapshot/screens/) §04 `typeRows` · §05 `radii` and `spacing`** |
| **Epic** | **Common Platform Foundation** — **[workflow 13](coordination/workflows.toml)** |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE**, 2026-08-10, on `main`. **Previously:** ✅ **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-10.** ✅ **All six of `Delivers`, plus a seventh the DoD requires and the measurement missed.** 📁 **`--text-title` 26px · `--text-metric` 30px · `--text-display` corrected 36→40px · `--radius-full` 999px · the dialog at `--radius-xl` 16px · card padding 20px on both axes.** ⚡ **The three added roles use Tailwind's TUPLE form** — 📁 §04 gives every role *size / leading / weight / tracking*, and **a size-only entry silently drops three of the four.** ⚠️ **`Metric`'s tabular half could NOT ride on the token**: `fontVariantNumeric` is not a `fontSize` option, so **`tabular-nums` is applied at the call site and that is recorded in the config rather than dropped silently.** 🔴 **A SEVENTH ROLE, announced not smuggled**: 📁 the DoD says *every role in §04's `typeRows`*, and **`Label` at 12.5px had no token** — `--text-xs` is 12px, half a pixel short. ⛔ **The row's measurement named three; this is a fourth**, and `--text-label` is added to satisfy the DoD as written. **If the office intended 12px to stand as faithful under [D-220](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §10, say so and it comes out.** ✅ **The row's named risk held**: `--text-lg` and `--text-sm` are NOT renamed, and a test asserts they are not. ⚡ **New coverage reads the DESIGN DOCUMENT, not a copy of its numbers** — 📁 [D-137](DECISION_REGISTER.md): *the copy is always the one somebody reads* — so a design refresh that changes a value fails the suite instead of passing it. ⚠️ **One guard's PARSER was widened and its contract was not**: `declaredScale` only matched `key: 'var(…)'` and the tuple form is `key: ['var(…)', {…}]`; **the value must still be a `var()`.** ✅ **Fire-tested — and the first attempt proved the wrong thing**: a literal alone changes nothing because the guard checks only steps a component USES. **2213 vitest / 109 files (+8, +1) · tsc 0 · eslint 0.** **Was: `CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-10** |
| ✅ **ANSWERED — `--text-label` at 12.5px STAYS** *(Lane C, 2026-08-10, on Lane A's question)* | 🔴 **Measured in the design's own data array, not reasoned from principle.** 📁 **[`Fortune Signora Design System v2.dc.html`](design-snapshot/screens/) `typeRows`, verbatim:** `['Label', '--text-label', '12.5 / 1.45 / 600', 12.5, 600, '-0.002em', 'Account owner']`. ⚡ **The token NAME and the VALUE are both the design's.** ⛔ **So it was never an addition beyond the measurement — the row's measurement was incomplete, and the implementation is faithful** |
| 🔴 **And 12px is not a rounding of it — it is a DIFFERENT ROLE** | 📁 **The line directly below Label in the same array:** `['Caption', '--text-caption', '12 / 1.5 / 400', 12, 400, '0', …]`. ⛔ **Label is 12.5 / 1.45 / **600**; Caption is 12 / 1.5 / **400** — different size, different line-height, different weight.** ⚡ **`--text-xs` at 12px is Caption's value.** 🔴 **Collapsing Label into it would merge two roles the design deliberately separates**, which is why [D-220](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §10's faithfulness rule REQUIRES 12.5 rather than merely permitting it. ✅ **Lane A undersold its own case: *half a pixel short* is a role conflation, not a rounding** |
| ⚠️ **One observation, not a demand** | 📁 **`--text-caption` is absent on the lane branch while the other eight roles are present.** ⛔ **Not raised as a defect and not a new requirement** — **the row is `IN VALIDATION` and this office does not add scope to a slice in flight** ([D-225](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §11). ✅ **Noted so the lane can decide, and QE judges the DoD as written** |
| 🔴 **This row corrects [D-218](DECISION_REGISTER.md), which said the token layer was exact** | ⚠️ **[D-217](DECISION_REGISTER.md) measured the COLOUR tokens and they ARE exact.** ⛔ **That was generalised to *"the token layer"* without testing the type scale, and the type scale does not match.** ⚡ **Recorded here rather than quietly fixed** ([§2](../CLAUDE.md)) |
| 🔴 **The measurement — `app/globals.css`:135–147 against `typeRows`** | ⛔ **`Display` — design **40**/1.05/700/−0.032em · `--text-display: 2.25rem` = **36px*** · ⛔ **`Title` — design **26**/1.15/700/−0.026em · NO TOKEN AT ANY NAME; nearest is `--text-xl` at 20px** · ⛔ **`Metric` — design **30**/1.05/700/**tabular** · NO TOKEN, and no tabular-numeral role anywhere** |
| ✅ **What matches and must NOT be renamed** | 📁 **`Section` 18px is `--text-lg: 1.125rem` ✅ · `Body` 14px is `--text-sm: 0.875rem` ✅.** ⛔ **Different internal names, faithful values** — 📁 **[D-220](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §10: *"if the implementation uses a different internal name but faithfully implements the design, do not create a task"* |
| ⚡ **Why `Title` and `Metric` are urgent rather than tidy** | 🔴 **`Title` at 26px IS the page header's title, and [TASK-1446](BACKLOG.md) is about to build that component** — ⛔ **it would be built against a scale missing the exact role it needs.** 🔴 **`Metric` at 30px with TABULAR numerals is every figure on the Executive Dashboard** — ⚡ **tabular numerals are what stop a column of currency jittering as it updates, which is a correctness property of a live figure, not a typographic preference** |
| ⚠️ **Three radius and spacing applications** | ⛔ **`dialog.tsx` is `rounded-lg` = 12px; §05 assigns the dialog `--r-xl` = 16px** · ⛔ **no `--radius-full` token exists; §05 needs 999px for avatar, status dot, count badge and progress track** · ⛔ **`card.tsx` is `px-5 py-4` — 20px horizontal and **16px vertical**, and §05 says card padding is *"20–24px — was 16px in v1"*.** ⚡ **The horizontal value migrated to v2 and the vertical one did not** |
| ✅ **The radius SCALE itself is exact and is not touched** | 📁 **4 · 6 · 8 · 12 · 16 in both.** ⛔ **This row fixes three call sites and adds one missing step; it does not re-derive the scale** |
| ⚡ **Delivers** | **`Title` and `Metric` as tokens with the design's size, leading, weight and tracking · `Metric` carrying tabular numerals · `Display` corrected to 40px · `--radius-full` · the dialog at 16px · card padding at the v2 value** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟠 **`app/globals.css` · `tailwind.config.ts` · `dialog.tsx` · `card.tsx`** — announce it |
| **Component coverage** | **`design-tokens.test.tsx` asserts each of the design's six type roles exists with the design's value** — ⛔ **a role absent at every name fails** · **the dialog's radius is asserted against the token, not a literal** |
| **Definition of Done** | **Every role in §04's `typeRows` exists as a token at the design's value, and the three applications match §05** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `product_surface` · ⛔ **no Playwright — nothing here is a journey** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | ⛔ **Renaming `--text-lg` and `--text-sm` to the design's words.** 🔴 **They are correct; renaming them is churn across the whole product and [D-220](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §10 explicitly forbids it** |

### TASK-1454 · The Playground seeder retires its predecessor without removing it

| | |
|---|---|
| **Classification** ([D-208](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §4) | ⚠️ **INCOMPLETE IMPLEMENTATION — a documented cleanup that does not clean up** |
| **Design authority** | ⚡ **None — repository tooling** |
| **Epic** | **Platform Foundation** |
| **Module** | **`platform_foundation`** — ⚡ **assigned 2026-08-09; `backend/tests/playground/` matched no module's paths until then** |
| **Status** | ⛔ **`DISPOSED` — Lane C, 2026-08-09, WITHDRAWN THE SAME DAY IT WAS WRITTEN. Not a defect.** ⚡ **The row is kept rather than deleted because the reason it was wrong is worth more than the row was** |
| 🔴 **Why it is withdrawn, and the retraction is mine to make** | ⛔ **The premise came from [B-228](BUGS.md), which Lane A has itself withdrawn: its whole investigation ran under an invocation that set `SIGNORA_TEST_APP_DATABASE_URL` and NOT `SIGNORA_TEST_ADMIN_DATABASE_URL`, so the ADMIN engine fell through to the default `signora` database.** 🔴 **`_retire_previous(admin_factory)` takes the ADMIN factory** — 📁 `seed.py`:267 — **so it queried and deleted from the wrong database and the real Playgrounds survived untouched.** ✅ **Under the correct invocation the seeder prints *"replacing 2 previous Playground(s)"* and exits 0.** ⚡ **The function does remove. It was never given the right database to remove from** |
| ⚠️ **What this row got wrong, stated plainly** | ⛔ **I accused `seed.py`'s docstring of describing a guarantee the code does not deliver, and cited [§2](../CLAUDE.md) at it.** ✅ **The code delivers the guarantee. The docstring is accurate.** ⛔ **I took a measurement from a broken environment and reasoned about the source as though the measurement were about the source** — ⚡ **which is the same class as the import-count claim [D-220](DECISION_REGISTER.md) caught, one layer further out: the evidence was real and it was evidence about something else** |
| ✅ **What is NOT withdrawn** | 📁 **The [`modules.toml`](coordination/modules.toml) registry fix stands on its own merits and Lane A says so independently**: `backend/tests/playground/` matched no module's `paths`, which was true before B-228 and would still be true if B-228 had never been filed |
| ⚠️ **Why the row exists when the function has a long comment about exactly this** | 📁 **`seed.py`'s own docstring describes the defect it was written to fix**: *"a database seeded twelve times held twelve organizations all named `Playground`… a reviewer opened the product and found an empty list, in a database with twelve full ones."* ⛔ **The `DELETE FROM workspace` is there and the rows survive it** — 🔴 **so the comment describes a guarantee the code does not deliver, which is the [§2](../CLAUDE.md) class this repository treats as worse than the absent version** |
| ⚡ **Delivers** | **A seeder whose second run leaves exactly one Playground** — ⛔ **and the reason the cascade did not reach these rows, named in the commit rather than worked around with a wider `DELETE`** |
| ⛔ **What it must NOT do** | 📁 **The function is deliberately scoped to its own `WORKSPACE_NAME` and deliberately relies on the schema's foreign keys** — *"nothing here knows the table list, which would be a second copy of the schema that drifts."* ⛔ **Do not enumerate tables to fix this** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`backend/tests/playground/`** |
| **Definition of Done** | **Seeding twice leaves one workspace, and a test proves it** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `platform_foundation` |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | ⛔ **Widening the `DELETE` until the count comes out right.** ✅ **The orphaned rows have a reason and the reason is the deliverable** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **n/a** |

| ⚡ **First deliverable: the reconciliation, before any production code changes** | 📁 **Trace the ACTUAL production path** — **authentication succeeds → session established → callback handling → auth redirect → application shell → workspace resolution → entry decision → destination route → Home composition → module content.** ⚡ **Then every alternative entry path**: normal sign-in · OAuth callback · invitation acceptance · onboarding completion · workspace creation · workspace switching · session expiry · deep links · step-up · locked account · deleted or suspended account · no workspace · single workspace · multiple workspaces · last-used · activation · first run · role-based Home · custom start page. ⛔ **The objective is to find EVERY place that makes or influences an entry decision** |

| ✅ **Classify every capability, evidence-based** ([D-224](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §5) | 🟢 **Correct and reusable — do not touch** · 🟡 **Partially compliant — precise correction task** · 🔴 **Incorrect — rework against the design** · ⚪ **Missing — implementation task** · 🔵 **UNVERIFIED — source suggests it exists and runtime evidence is required.** ⛔ **A 🔵 may never be reported as compliant** |
| 🔴 **Reuse before reimplementation** ([D-224](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §8) | ⛔ **Inspect existing routes, components, services, hooks, state, auth callbacks, redirect logic, workspace logic, Home/dashboard, shell and tests, and map each design requirement to its actual implementation — BEFORE deciding what new work is needed.** ⚡ **This repository has repeatedly created a task for something that already existed elsewhere** |
| ⛔ **Do not enhance an incorrect implementation** ([D-224](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §6) | ⛔ **No layer around a wrong component · no compatibility behaviour the design does not specify · no old navigation kept just in case · no second implementation beside the old one · no merge of old and new · no invented intermediate behaviour** |
| ⛔ **Do not invent missing behaviour** ([D-224](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §7) | **Where the design does not specify something, STOP AT THAT AMBIGUITY and raise it.** ⚡ **Surgically** — ⛔ **one unresolved question does not block the rest of the audit.** **Lane C routes it to the Design Authority or the product owner** |
| ⚡ **Runtime is Lane A's half** ([D-224](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §10) | **Browser rendering · navigation · interaction · authentication · workspace state · redirect behaviour · visual composition · route transitions.** ⛔ **Lane C has no runtime and its source inspection does not prove any of them** |
| 📁 **Required output** ([D-224](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §14) | **A table: Area · Design requirement · Existing implementation · Status · Action** — **over entry engine · redirect ownership · workspace resolution · Home route · Executive / Seller / RevOps compositions · activation · deep links · session expiry · step-up · invitation · onboarding exit.** 🔴 **Every *compliant* claim carries evidence · every *missing* claim names what was searched · every *incorrect* claim cites the design requirement AND the conflicting implementation · every *ambiguous* item names exactly what the design does not specify** |
| **Change Budget** | ⚡ **The audit itself touches no production code.** **Implementation scope is set BY the audit and announced then** |
| **Definition of Done** | **The reconciliation table exists, every row carries its evidence class, and the implementation work it justifies has been created** — ⛔ **implementation of the corrections is NOT in this row; it is whatever the audit produces** |

| 🔴 **§16 · the third loading tier** | 📁 ***"In-place refreshes show a 2px bar under the top bar and dim stale figures to 60%; they never cover data that is already readable."*** ⛔ **No `h-[2px]`/`h-0.5` bar and no `opacity-60` exists in `components/shell/`.** ✅ **Tiers one and two — cold boot paints chrome and skeletons content, route changes keep the shell — appear satisfied by `Skeleton` and `RegionBoundary`; VERIFY before building, and build only tier three** |
| 🔴 **§18 · the empty workspace** | 📁 ***"the full shell with real navigation, not a stripped-down wizard: destinations that need data are visible but marked… a three-step activation checklist with live counts."*** ⛔ **No activation checklist exists.** ⚡ **A brand-new workspace is the first screen every customer sees, and the design is explicit that it is not a wizard** |
| 🔴 **§19 · workspace-labelled surfaces** | 📁 ***"Every workspace-scoped surface — search, notifications, filters, the assistant — is labelled with the workspace it belongs to whenever more than one is available to the user."*** ⛔ **Neither `shell-palette.tsx` nor `copilot.tsx` reads `activeWorkspace`.** 🔴 **A user in two workspaces cannot tell which one the palette is searching** — 📁 §19 calls the workspace *"the hard boundary of data, permission and configuration"* |
| ⚠️ **§19 is conditional and the condition matters** | ⛔ ***"whenever more than one is available to the user"*** — ✅ **a single-workspace user must NOT see the label.** ⚡ **`useWorkspaceContext` already exposes `workspaces`, so the condition is available and must not be approximated** |
| ✅ **Four of the seven need nothing — verified, so nobody re-audits them** | 📁 **§05 Breadcrumbs**: ellipsis, `aria-current` and the status dot are all in `breadcrumb.tsx`. 📁 **§07 Banners**: `ShellBanners` stacks, and **system status is correctly a pill in the top bar rather than a banner** — the design's sharpest distinction here, and the implementation gets it right. 📁 **§17 Errors**: `error-state.tsx` carries the copyable correlation ID; `RegionBoundary` keeps failures inside the shell. 📁 **§21 Accessibility**: landmarks present — ⚠️ **except the duplicate `Primary`, which is [TASK-1461](#task-1461)'s**, and the label conflict, which is [C-1422](LANE_COORDINATION.md)'s |
| 🔴 **What it must NOT do** | ⛔ **Do not build tiers one and two of §16 without measuring them first.** ⛔ **Do not label a single-workspace user's surfaces.** ⛔ **Do not touch the search `aria-label`** — [C-1422](LANE_COORDINATION.md) is with the product owner. ⛔ **Do not build the filter strip or utility panel** — [TASK-1432](#task-1432), [TASK-1433](#task-1433) |

| ♿ **Accessibility** | **One primary landmark at every width · trigger keyboard-reachable and labelled · focus trapped in the open sheet and restored to the trigger on close** |
| **Definition of Done** | **A customer below 1024 opens navigation from the top bar and it arrives as a sheet** · **the bottom bar does not exist in the codebase** · **certified · `main`** |

| **Module** | **`product_surface` — Lane A** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`READY` — Lane C, 2026-08-10, from the UI foundation reconciliation** |

| 🔴 **The measurement** | ⛔ **`grid-cols-12` appears NOWHERE in `frontend/`.** ⛔ **`1600` appears only as a BREAKPOINT (`tailwind.config.ts`:39) and never as a content maximum.** ⚡ **There is no `Grid`, `Container`, `PageLayout` or `Stack` in a 38-component `components/ui/` library** |
| ⚠️ **What screens do instead** | 📁 **Seven different width models, invented per screen**: `max-w-3xl` ×9 · `max-w-md` ×5 · `max-w-sm` ×4 · `max-w-card` ×3 · `max-w-[57…]` ×2 · `max-w-xl` · `max-w-[35rem]`. ⛔ **The dominant one is `max-w-3xl` — 768px.** ⚡ **[43 §4a](architecture/43-customer-experience.md) already described the product this way in its own words: *"a single `max-w-3xl` column of stacked cards"*** |
| 🔴 **Why this is the foundation finding** | ⛔ **A 768px centred column and a 12-column grid to 1600px are two different layout models, not two settings of one.** ⚡ **On a 1600px screen the current model uses 48% of the width** — which is what *"components designed for desktop but squeezed"*, *"layouts not adapting"* and *"spacing inconsistent"* look like from the outside. ✅ **Fix this once and the screen-level symptoms resolve together; fix them per screen and the eighth screen invents an eighth width** |
| ✅ **NOT a rebuild of the token layer — it is verified correct** | 🔴 **This row's most important line.** ⚡ **The colour tokens carry Design System v2's EXACT values** — `--primary 203 72% 27%` → `rgb(19 78 118)` = `--color-accent`, and six others match to ±3/255. ⛔ **Do not renumber, rename or "migrate" the palette; it is already the finalized one.** ✅ **Same for the four breakpoints** |
| ⚡ **Delivers** | **One layout primitive in `components/ui/`** implementing the design's grid — **12 columns · 24px gutters · 1600px content maximum** — and **the shell's content region composed through it.** ⛔ **Migration of the seven ad-hoc widths is NOT in this row** — see *Sequencing* |
| 🔴 **What it must NOT do** | ⛔ **Do not touch `globals.css` colour tokens** (verified correct). ⛔ **Do not change the four breakpoints** (verified correct). ⛔ **Do not migrate screens in this slice** — one primitive, one consumer, then measure. 📁 **[455](architecture/455-context-definitions-r02-r03.md) §4: *"a port with seven simultaneous first callers has no chance of being corrected cheaply"*** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟠 **`components/ui/` gains a component — announce it** · 🟢 `app-shell.tsx`'s content region · 🟢 its tests |
| **Playwright coverage** | ⚠️ **Blocked on [B-428](BUGS.md) for the responsive half** — the matrix runs at 1280 only. ✅ **What IS assertable today: content maximum 1600px and 24px gutters at 1280, by `boundingBox()`** |
| **Definition of Done** | **One primitive, composed into the shell, with the design's three numbers asserted from geometry rather than class names** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚠️ **Runtime status** | ⛔ **LEVEL 1/2 NOT VERIFIED.** **This row is source-derived.** ⚡ **No authenticated session was available to Lane C**; the visual claim is the design's, and the measurements above are of the code |

| 🔴 **Sized against `origin/integration`, not against the design document** ([D-215](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | ⚡ **This is the first row written under that commitment, and the sizing is shown rather than asserted.** ✅ **Exists today**: `lib/state/workspace-provider.tsx` · `app/(app)/authenticated-shell.tsx` mounting it · `app/(app)/layout.tsx` · the whole `(app)` group. ⛔ **Does not exist**: any `app/**/w/` directory — `find` returns nothing. ✅ **So this row moves a mount point and changes one source of truth.** ⛔ **It does not build a workspace context, a shell, or a Home** |

| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`components/shell/**`** · ⛔ **`lib/shell/scope.ts` is not re-decided here** |
| **Definition of Done** | **Setting a chip changes what at least one module renders** · **certified · `main`** |

| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | ⛔ **Shipping it "ready for later".** ✅ **This row's whole reason is that a strip without a reader is worse than no strip** |

| ⚡ **Delivers** | **All five call sites render through `SystemState`.** ⛔ **No new component, no visual redesign** — 📁 the ladder and its 23 tests already ship |
| 🔴 **One of the five is anonymous today** | 📁 **Design System v2 `1.3.1`: *"an h1 that names what happened — not 'Error'."*** ⛔ **That is a live Design System violation in the shell, and it is the strongest argument for doing this now rather than at leisure** |
| **Change Budget** | ✅ **`components/shell/app-shell.tsx` is covered by the UI Migration Program** — [CLAUDE.md](../CLAUDE.md) line 429, **a standing approved architectural change; no fresh grant and no ADR** · 🟢 the four surfaces. ⚠️ **The exemption is directional, not blanket** — ⛔ **it covers changes *toward* the finalized Design System and nothing else; a convenience edit to `app-shell.tsx` inside this row is still 🔴** |
| **Playwright coverage** | **A failure at each of the four surfaces renders the ladder's shape, with a copyable identifier and a bounded retry** |
| **Component coverage** | 🔴 **No surface renders an anonymous `Error` heading** · **every failure carries a retry budget** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | **Politeness by scope, inherited from `SystemState`** — ⛔ **not re-decided per surface** |
| 📱 **Responsive behaviour** | ✅ **Unchanged** |
| **Definition of Done** | 📁 **[TASK-1409](BACKLOG.md)'s own failure test is satisfied: no surface writes a local 404** · **certified · `main`** |

### TASK-1428 · One failed-read primitive — `StatedAbsence` becomes the ladder's region scope

| | |
|---|---|
| 🔴 **Why this exists** | ⛔ **[C-252](LANE_COORDINATION.md): two published primitives own one concern and both are correct in isolation.** ⚡ **Found by [TASK-445](BACKLOG.md)'s own guard failing on [TASK-1409](BACKLOG.md)'s new boundary — the guard working exactly as written** |
| **Classification** ([D-208](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §4) | ⚠️ **CONTRADICTORY DESIGN, resolved by composition rather than by choosing a winner** |
| **Epic** | **System States** — **[workflow 28](coordination/workflows.toml)** |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`CLAIMED` — Lane A**, 2026-08-09, by the Task Claim Protocol, **on the product owner's direction to proceed**. ⚠️ **Claimed from `origin/main`, not `origin/integration`** — the row exists only on `main`, and `integration` is 31 commits behind and cannot fast-forward ([B-161](BUGS.md)/[B-220](BUGS.md)). ⛔ **Step 7 could not be performed and the reason is recorded rather than forced.** *(Previously* ✅ **`READY` — Lane C, 2026-08-09, ruling [C-252](LANE_COORDINATION.md).**.*)* |
| 🔴 **The ruling, and it adopts Lane A's recommendation** | ✅ **`StatedAbsence` becomes a CALLER of `SystemState` at region scope.** ⛔ **Neither is deleted and neither wins.** ⚡ **It keeps the sentence — the thing nine local copies proved valuable — and gains the scope, the three-attempt budget and the copyable correlation id from the ladder** |
| ⚠️ **And the correction is this office's, recorded rather than absorbed** | 📁 **[TASK-1409](BACKLOG.md)'s row said *"absence is 'there is nothing', a system state is 'something failed', and conflating them is how a broken widget renders as an empty one."*** 🔴 **That distinction does not hold against the code.** 📁 **`stated-absence.tsx:79` composes *"Signora could not load {subject}"* and its test asserts it *"never blames the account for a failure of ours"* — ⛔ **it is a FAILED READ and always was.** ✅ **Lane A read the component instead of the row, which is [C-428](LANE_COORDINATION.md)'s rule doing its job on a row this office wrote** |
| ✅ **Measured, not assumed** | 📁 **`components/feedback/stated-absence.tsx:42` — `StatedAbsence({ subject })`, `subject` has no default *"a caller cannot say nothing"*.** 📁 **`components/ui/system-state.tsx:71` — `SystemStateProps { scope, state, correlationId }`, politeness derived from scope, and it **throws** if a state is not one the scope owns |
| ⚡ **Delivers** | **One failed-read path.** **`StatedAbsence` keeps its signature and its sentence** — ⛔ **no caller changes** — **and renders through `SystemState` with `scope="region"`.** ✅ **Retry, attempt count and the correlation id arrive at all nine existing call sites for free** |
| 🔴 **What it must NOT do** | ⛔ **Reword either sentence to satisfy the guard.** 📁 **Lane A's own note: *"rewording to dodge a regex is precisely the fork the guard exists to prevent."*** ✅ **The guard stays as written and this row makes it pass by composition** |
| **Reusable components required** | **`SystemState`** — ⛔ **and if its region scope will not carry `StatedAbsence`'s sentence, that is a finding against the ladder, not a licence to keep two** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟠 **`components/feedback/stated-absence.tsx`** — **a delivered, guarded primitive with nine callers; announced in the commit** · 🟢 **`components/ui/system-state.tsx`** |
| **Playwright coverage** | ✅ **The nine existing `StatedAbsence` call sites render unchanged text** — 🔴 **that assertion is the whole safety of this row** |
| **Component coverage** | **A region failure renders the same way whether a read returned an error or a component threw** — ⛔ **that divergence is the defect** · **the guard still fails on a tenth local *"could not load"*** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | **Politeness comes from the scope and never from the caller** — ⚡ **already true of `SystemState` and this row inherits it rather than re-deciding it** |
| 📱 **Responsive behaviour** | ✅ **Unchanged — the region boundary inherits its container** |
| **Definition of Done** | **One failed-read primitive, nine callers unchanged, and `TASK-1409`'s region boundary restored** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `product_surface` · **Security Invariant** ⛔ **a correlation id is an opaque identifier and never carries tenant data** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | ⛔ **Treating this as a merge of two components.** ✅ **It is one composing the other: `StatedAbsence` owns the SENTENCE, the ladder owns the SCOPE, and each keeps what it was good at** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **n/a** |

### TASK-1427 · The signal queue publishes the two facts its screen already draws

| | |
|---|---|
| 🔴 **Why this exists — and it is this office's defect** | ⛔ **[TASK-439](#task-439--the-signal-center--the-triage-queue-a-seller-actually-works)'s `Delivers` names five fields the design draws, and [C-251](LANE_COORDINATION.md) measured that the published contract carries three.** ⚡ **Lane A found it after the L-1 reuse audit and BEFORE writing a line, and refused to render `why_it_matters` as the summary — 📁 *"it would look right and be wrong on every row"*, the same sentence under fifty different signals** |
| **Classification** ([D-208](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §4) | ✅ **MISSING IMPLEMENTATION — and much smaller than it looked.** ⛔ **Not a missing capability and not a design question: BOTH FACTS ALREADY EXIST ON THE DOMAIN OBJECT and the response schema does not publish them** |
| **Epic** | **Signal Center** — **[workflow 21](coordination/workflows.toml)** |
| **Module** | **`signal_engine`** — **Lane A's in [`modules.toml`](coordination/modules.toml)** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE**, 2026-08-10, on `main`. **Previously:** 🟣 **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-09.** ✅ **Delivered: the queue draws four of the design's five fields from published data** — `rationale`, per signal and never per type. ✅ **Journey written AND executed**: `signal-center` **4/4 green** against a freshly seeded environment, including the rewritten order-independent walk. ⚠️ **It also fails inside the full 218 suite, and that is [B-229](BUGS.md) rather than this slice** — `organization-setup` [140/218] creates a workspace and restores nothing, so every account-backed journey after it reads an empty one; **measured, and the spec has no `afterAll`.** ⛔ **The confidence figure is ABSENT BY DECISION, not omission** — [C-267](LANE_COORDINATION.md): `Relevance` is a category its own docstring says is *"never computed"*, the only mapping is `buying_score_engine`'s and **workspace-overridable**, and `confidence.tsx` cannot render absence without printing the `0 of 5` the row forbids |
| 📁 **Measured on `origin/integration`, not assumed** | **`domain/signal.py:81` — `rationale: str`**, and its own comment is the design's sentence: 📁 ***"Two halves: why this matters HERE, and what it rests on. Rendered together so the interpretation a customer reads and the evidence an auditor checks cannot diverge."*** ⚡ **That IS the design's per-row `summary`.** **`domain/signal.py:64` — `relevance: Relevance`**, the per-instance strength. ⛔ **`interface/triage_schemas.py:86 QueueEntryResponse` publishes neither** |
| ⚡ **Delivers** | **Two fields on `QueueEntryResponse`** — **`rationale`, and the confidence figure derived from `relevance`** — **plus the same two on the frontend type.** ⛔ **No new domain concept, no new table, no new query** |
| 🔴 **The reuse finding, and it is the point of writing this row rather than a component** | 📁 **`frontend/components/ui/confidence.tsx` EXISTS, renders *"confidence 4 of 5"*, and is imported by NOTHING.** ⚡ **It is [TASK-1411](#task-1411--the-ai-attribution-primitive--one-confidence-one-source-list-one-show-reasoning)'s primitive, already half-built and orphaned.** ⛔ **This row uses it and does not write a second one** — **and if its shape does not fit, that is a finding against [TASK-1411](#task-1411--the-ai-attribution-primitive--one-confidence-one-source-list-one-show-reasoning), not a licence to fork** |
| ⚠️ **What it must NOT do** | ⛔ **Render `why_it_matters` as the summary.** 📁 **The catalog's own comment: it is a fact about the TYPE, true before any account exhibits it.** ✅ **Lane A's refusal is upheld and recorded here so nobody re-decides it** |
| **Reusable components required** | **`components/ui/confidence.tsx`** — ⛔ **use it; do not fork it** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟠 **`backend/modules/signal_engine/interface/**`** — **a response schema change, announced in the commit** · 🟢 **`frontend/lib/api/types.ts` and the queue row** |
| **Playwright coverage** | **A queue row renders its own rationale, and two rows of the same `signal_type` render DIFFERENT summaries** — 🔴 **that assertion is the whole defect, stated as a test** |
| **Component coverage** | **A signal with no confidence renders absence, never `0 of 5`** — ⛔ **[TASK-1411](#task-1411--the-ai-attribution-primitive--one-confidence-one-source-list-one-show-reasoning)'s rule, and `0 of 5` reads as *certainly wrong* where the truth is *not known*** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | **The confidence figure has a text equivalent** — 📁 *"confidence 4 of 5"*, never a bare graphic |
| 📱 **Responsive behaviour** | **The summary clamps to two lines with the full text available** — ⛔ **a truncated rationale must not read as the whole reason** |
| **Definition of Done** | **The queue draws all five of the design's fields from published data** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `signal_engine` · **Routes** the triage queue · **APIs** `QueueEntryResponse` · **Security Invariant** ⛔ **`rationale` is workspace-scoped tenant data and crosses only through `public/`** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | ⛔ **Deriving a summary in the frontend from the fields already present.** ✅ **`rationale` is authored where the reasoning happens; recomposing it in a component would put the interpretation a customer reads out of step with the evidence an auditor checks — which is exactly what the domain comment forbids** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **n/a — Signora's own reasoning** |

Reinhardt"*) and NOTHING publishes them.** ⛔ **Raised as [C-1403](LANE_COORDINATION.md) rather than invented.** ✅ **The request-access action is a write `workspace_tenancy` does not have** — **the row ships the state without the request button and says so**, rather than faking an affordance ([D-167](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §4) |
| **Reusable components required** | ⚡ **This row IS the reusable component.** ✅ **`<ErrorBoundary scope=…>` is what every later workflow consumes; [TASK-445](#task-445--stated-absence-becomes-one-implementation-instead-of-nine-local-ones) already built *stated absence* and the two must not fork** — ⛔ **absence is "there is nothing", a system state is "something failed", and conflating them is how a broken widget renders as an empty one |

| **Playwright coverage** | **A route that does not exist renders `404` with the shell retained** · **a forbidden route names the missing ROLE and no resource detail** · 🔴 **and the one that matters: a component throwing inside a region leaves the rest of the page interactive** — asserted by mounting a deliberately throwing child |
| **Component coverage** | **Each of the four scopes renders its own legal states and refuses the others** · **the retry control retries and reports attempt N of 3** · **`req_…` identifiers are copyable** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | **Every state has `role="alert"` or `role="status"` by severity** · **focus moves to the state's heading when it replaces content** · ⛔ **colour is never the only signal** — the design pairs every one with an icon and a sentence |
| 📱 **Responsive behaviour** | **The full-page states are single-column below `sm` with the action stack becoming vertical** · **the region boundary inherits its container and never introduces a horizontal scroll** |

| ✅ **Verification strategy** | ⚡ **Thirteen workflows stop needing their own error handling.** ⛔ **If a later workflow still writes a local 404, this row was built as four screens rather than as a ladder** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | 🔴 **The temptation is to build four pages.** ⛔ **Four pages satisfy a screenshot review and leave every region-scope failure unhandled — which is the case that actually reaches customers, because a whole-page 500 is rare and a widget that cannot load is daily** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **n/a — Signora's own surface** |

keeping**: **the lane fire-tested its own enforcement and it was guarded by nothing** — *removing `freshAge: 600` left 2158 tests passing, the dialog still rendering, and the enforcement window silently a day.* ✅ **`600` is read from Security Center §02, not chosen**, and **`action` is required with no default** because *"'confirm your identity' with no object is how people approve things they did not intend."* ⏸️ **`BLOCKED` (its first caller) — Lane A, 2026-08-09.** ✅ **Delivered and green**: `session.freshAge = 600` — 📁 the design’s *"step-up lasts 10 minutes, short enough that an unlocked laptop is not a standing authorisation"*, against Better Auth’s 24-hour default — `CodeInput` in `components/ui`, and `StepUpDialog`, which **names what it authorises** because 📁 *"confirm your identity with no object is how people approve things they did not intend"*. 🔴 **The SERVER half is guarded after a fire-test found it was not**: removing `freshAge` left 2158 tests green while the window silently became a day — **the row’s own warning arriving by the route it names.** ⛔ **NO CALLER, and it is a design fact rather than a shortfall**: 📁 [C-265](LANE_COORDINATION.md) — **measured across all twenty-eight screens, exactly one module requires a fresh factor** (Security Center §02, *"every control in THIS MODULE"*), **and it is workflow 23, unbuilt.** ⚠️ **Team Management’s `useRemoveMember` was NOT wired**: the design does not mark it, and marking it would be inventing a security requirement ✅ **`CLAIMED` — Lane A**, 2026-08-09, **the challenge is built and no caller invokes it yet.** ✅ **Delivered**: `session.freshAge = 600` — 📁 the design’s *"step-up lasts 10 minutes, short enough that an unlocked laptop is not a standing authorisation"*, read rather than chosen, against Better Auth’s 24-hour default — `CodeInput` in `components/ui`, and `StepUpDialog`, which **names what it is about to authorise** because 📁 *"confirm your identity with no object is how people approve things they did not intend"*. 🔴 **A fire-test found the SERVER half unprotected**: removing `freshAge` left **2158 tests green** while the window silently became a day — **the row’s own warning, *a step-up that only guards the dialog guards nothing*, arriving by the route it names.** ✅ **Now guarded, and the guard fails three ways.** ⛔ **No caller yet**: `resolveEntry` returns `STEPUP_REQUIRED` and no destructive action invokes the challenge, so the Definition of Done is not met ✅ **`CLAIMED` — Lane A**, 2026-08-09, by the Task Claim Protocol. ✅ **The row says BUY **`better-auth@1.6.25` ships `session.freshAge` — default `3600 * 24` — and its session route raises `SESSION_NOT_FRESH`.** ⚡ **So the server-side enforcement the Security Invariant demands already exists and is configured, not built** *(Was:* ✅ **`READY` — Lane C, 2026-08-09***)* |
| **Design authority** | ⛔ **`Fortune Signora Security Center.dc.html` §02** — the *"Confirm it is you"* dialog |
