| **Component coverage** | **The expiry counts down and the challenge expires** · **backup codes are accepted once** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | **The dialog traps focus and returns it to the invoking control** — ⚠️ **[B-312](BUGS.md) is an open defect for exactly this class and must not be repeated** · **the countdown is announced politely, not assertively** |
| 📱 **Responsive behaviour** | **Full-screen sheet below `sm`; centred dialog above** |
| **Definition of Done** | **`resolveEntry` returning `STEPUP_REQUIRED` reaches a working challenge, and a destructive action cannot complete without one** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `product_surface` · **Routes** the challenge · **APIs** Better Auth · **Security Invariant** 🔴 **the challenge is enforced SERVER-SIDE — [§20c](../CLAUDE.md), a client check is a courtesy, and a step-up that only guards the dialog guards nothing** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | ⛔ **Building this as a UI dialog.** ✅ **It is an authorization boundary that happens to have a dialog** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **BUY — Better Auth, already ratified** ([09-build-vs-buy.md](architecture/09-build-vs-buy.md)) |

### TASK-1411 · The AI attribution primitive — one confidence, one source list, one *Show reasoning*

| | |
|---|---|
| 🔴 **Found across three designs, and it is the same component** | ⚡ **Account 360: *"Show reasoning · 6 sources · Confidence 4 of 5 · Generated 09:14 · updates on new evidence"*.** **Reports: *"Confidence 4 of 5 · 3 sources · Regenerate"*.** **AI Research: *"v3 · 14 sources · 3 excluded · Confidence 4 of 5"*.** ⛔ **Three workflows reached separately build three, and they will disagree about what *4 of 5* means** |
| **Epic** | **AI Platform surface** — serves **[workflows 15, 22, 25](coordination/workflows.toml)** |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** reading **`ai_platform`**'s `public/` |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE**, 2026-08-10, in certified `6c42af4`. **Previously:** 🔵 **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-10.** ✅ **Built**: `components/ui/attribution.tsx` — **`Show reasoning` · a source count that opens the sources · `N excluded` · confidence as `N of 5` · `Generated <day>`**, ⛔ **all four wrapping and none truncating.** 🔴 **Confidence renders its ABSENCE**, as the row instructs, because 📁 **`ai_platform/public/` publishes none — never `0 of 5`**, and ⛔ **not `StatedAbsence` either**: that sentence ends *"reload to try again"*, which would be a false instruction where nothing failed. 🔴 **The Security Invariant holds and was fire-tested**: **`readable: false` leaves the list AND the count.** ✅ **`Confidence` gains its first production caller** ([C-266](LANE_COORDINATION.md)). ✅ **First consumer is `NarrativeBlock`** — Account 360's summary block. ⚠️ **The Playwright requirement is NOT met and is recorded rather than claimed**: ⛔ **there is no Reports screen, and `ai_narration` holds 0 rows in `signora_lane_a`**, so neither of the two screens has a runtime instance to visit. ✅ **12 component tests, all states; suite 2503 green; tsc 0; eslint 0.** **Was: `CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-10** |
| **Design authority** | ⛔ **`Account 360`, `Reports` and `AI Research` `.dc.html` — all three, because the component must satisfy all three** |
| ✅ **What it delivers** | **A confidence expressed as *N of 5* and never as a percentage** — 📁 all three designs agree · **a source count that OPENS the sources** (*"Open all evidence"*, *"3 excluded"*) · **a generated-at time** · **and *Show reasoning*, which is the disclosure the product's honesty principle rests on** |
| 🔴 **What it must NOT do** | ⛔ **Render a confidence the model did not produce.** ⚠️ **[TASK-416](#task-416--an-evaluation-run-states-which-provider-served-it) already established that a run states which provider served it** — **if `ai_platform` publishes no confidence, this component renders its ABSENCE** ([TASK-445](#task-445--stated-absence-becomes-one-implementation-instead-of-nine-local-ones)), it does not compute one |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | ⚠️ **Whether `ai_platform.public` exposes confidence and source provenance is UNVERIFIED and the row says so** — ✅ **the first slice is the read, and if the contract is missing it is raised, not invented** ([D-167](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §4) |
| **Reusable components required** | ⛔ **`3 excluded` in AI Research means the source list has an EXCLUDED state** — **the component carries it from the start rather than being widened later by the third consumer** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`components/ui`** |
| **Playwright coverage** | **The same component renders on Account 360 and on a report with the same markup** |
| **Component coverage** | **`N of 5` with no confidence renders *absence*, never `0 of 5`** — 🔴 **`0 of 5` reads as *certainly wrong* where the truth is *not known*** · **the source list opens and an excluded source is visibly excluded with its reason** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | ***Confidence 4 of 5* is the accessible name** — ⛔ **not a bare graphic** · ***Show reasoning* is a disclosure button with `aria-expanded`** |
| 📱 **Responsive behaviour** | **The attribution strip wraps rather than truncating** — ⛔ **a truncated source count is a false claim about evidence** |
| **Definition of Done** | **One component, three consumers, and no local copy** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `product_surface`, `ai_platform` · **Security Invariant** ⛔ **a source the reader may not open is not listed** — **the same disclosure rule as [C-245](LANE_COORDINATION.md)** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | 🔴 **Building it for Account 360 and generalising later.** ⛔ **That is how the three copies happen; the row exists because the generalisation is knowable NOW** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **n/a — Signora's own surface** |

### TASK-1412 · Workflow 11 · Settings — workspace identity, users and roles

| | |
|---|---|
| **Epic** | **Settings** — **[workflow 11](coordination/workflows.toml)** |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** reading **`workspace_tenancy`**'s `public/` |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE**, 2026-08-10, on `main`. **Previously:** 🔵 **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-10.** 🔴 **The slice is the one thing that was missing: the audit log had data and NO DOOR.** ✅ **`/settings/audit` renders `GET /workspaces/{id}/history`** — **`previous_role`/`new_role` are the design's *before and after values*** — **and its card ships on `/settings` in the same commit**, 📁 which is the CX-01 rule `settings/page.tsx` itself states. ⛔ **`OrganizationHistory` is REUSED, not copied**: a second renderer is how two surfaces start disagreeing about what a role change looked like. ⚠️ **Measured before building**: **the identity block already exists at `/settings/organization`** (TASK-468) — ⛔ **a second is the duplicate work §21 forbids, the same objection Lane C accepted for the roster** — ✅ **so the two missing `workspaceFields` were added THERE, locked with reasons.** ⛔ **And there is no workspace rename to attribute**: `workspace_tenancy` publishes no `PATCH` on `/workspaces/{id}`, so no `Edit` control was drawn over nothing. 🔴 **No retention figure is printed** — *7 years* would be mock business data on the one screen whose value is that it invents nothing. ✅ **7 new tests including both halves of P-A; 2539 frontend green; tsc 0; eslint 0; route verified at runtime.** **Was: `CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-10** |
| **Design authority** | ⛔ **`docs/design-snapshot/screens/Fortune Signora Settings.dc.html`** |
| ✅ **What it delivers** | **Workspace identity, region and fiscal calendar** (📁 *"Changing region requires support"* — **a stated limitation, not a disabled control**) · **the user list with `Provisioned by` and the SCIM read-only rule** · **roles and permissions as an additive matrix** |
| 🔴 **The design carries three findings and they are the row's substance** | 📁 ***"Three settings need attention"*** — **SCIM deprovisioning off while SSO is enforced · two API keys unrotated for a year · a role granting export on data its holders cannot see.** ⚡ **These are not decoration: the third is an authorization defect the product SHOWS, which means the platform must be able to DETECT it** |
| ⛔ **Scope, bounded** | **This row is identity, users and roles.** ⛔ **API keys and webhooks are [TASK-1413](#task-1413--api-keys-and-webhooks--scoped-rotatable-and-logged-per-call); SSO and SCIM configuration is [TASK-493](#task-493--better-auth-plugins--four-capabilities-one-file)'s plugins; billing is [workflow 24](coordination/workflows.toml)** |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | ✅ **[TASK-471](#task-471--workflow-10--team-management-to-the-approved-design) builds the member directory** — 🔴 **and [D-189](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §3 already recorded that TASK-471's directory and TASK-466's bulk table are the SAME data table.** ⛔ **This user list is the third caller; whichever lands first owns it** |
| **Reusable components required** | **The shared data table** · **[TASK-1409](#task-1409--workflow-28--system-states--the-scope-ladder-and-a-failure-that-claims-the-smallest-surface-it-can)'s region boundary for each section** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`frontend/app/(app)/settings/**`** |
| **Playwright coverage** | **An admin changes workspace identity and the audit log records it** · 🔴 **a SCIM-provisioned user cannot be edited locally** — **the design's stated reason is drift, and the assertion is that the control is absent rather than disabled-with-a-tooltip** |
| **Component coverage** | **The permission matrix renders additively and an inconsistent grant is flagged with its consequence in a sentence** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | **The matrix is a real `<table>` with row and column headers** — ⛔ **a grid of icons is unreadable to a screen reader** · **each warning is associated with its row via `aria-describedby`** |
| 📱 **Responsive behaviour** | **The matrix scrolls horizontally inside its own container** — ⛔ **never the page** · **section cards stack below `md`** |
| **Definition of Done** | **Identity, users and roles render and every change is attributed in the audit log** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `product_surface`, `workspace_tenancy` · **APIs** membership and role reads · **Security Invariant** 🔴 **a role change is written through the same Casbin enforcer that evaluates it** — ⛔ **never a second path** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | ⛔ **The design shows a workspace with SSO, SCIM, 312 seats and five roles. None of that exists.** ✅ **The row renders what the platform can answer and states the rest as absent** — **the class this lane has now refused six times** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **n/a for the surface; SSO/SCIM are Better Auth, ratified** |

### TASK-1413 · API keys and webhooks — scoped, rotatable, and logged per call

| | |
|---|---|
| 🔴 **A platform capability, found inside a settings screen** | 📁 **The Settings design: *"Scoped, rotatable, and logged per call"* · *"A key is shown once at creation and never again"* · *"Failed webhook deliveries retry with backoff for 24 hours, then park with the payload retained."*** ⛔ **None of that exists.** ⚡ **It is a credential system and an outbound delivery system wearing a settings section's clothes** |
| **Epic** | **Platform Foundation** |
| **Module** | 🔴 **UNASSIGNED — [C-1404](LANE_COORDINATION.md).** ⛔ **No module owns machine credentials.** ⚠️ **Raised rather than assigned, because inventing a module boundary is architecture** ([§21](../CLAUDE.md)) |
| **Status** | ⏸️ **`SEQUENCED` — behind [C-1404](LANE_COORDINATION.md)**, which is Product Office's own and answerable this week |
| **Design authority** | ⛔ **`Fortune Signora Settings.dc.html` §*API keys & webhooks*** |
| ✅ **What it delivers** | **A key issued once, stored hashed, never re-displayed** · **a scope narrower than its creator's** · **rotation with an overlap window** · **a per-call log** · **webhook delivery with backoff and a parked-payload state** |
| 🔴 **Why it matters more than its screen** | ⚡ **[TASK-1407](#task-1407--the-engine-admits-a-non-human-principal-before-one-exists) widens the entry engine to admit a NON-HUMAN principal.** ⛔ **This row is where non-human principals come from.** ✅ **The two must agree on what a service principal is, and [495](architecture/495-principal-and-identity-boundary.md) already ratified the model** |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | ✅ **[TASK-492](#task-492--the-security-event-stream--one-append-only-record-every-module-writes)'s security event stream is where *"logged per call"* belongs** — ⛔ **not a second log** · ✅ **the Action Batch's append-only pattern ([TASK-463](#task-463--the-action-batchs-persistence--append-only-and-no-update-grant)) is the model for the delivery record** |
| **Change Budget** | 🔴 **a new module or a new bounded area — requires an ADR** ([§21](../CLAUDE.md)) |
| **Playwright coverage** | **A key is created, shown once, and is not retrievable afterwards** · **rotation leaves the old key valid for the overlap and then not** |
| **Component coverage** | **A failed delivery parks with its payload and can be replayed** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | **The one-time key is selectable text with an explicit copy control and a spoken confirmation** — ⛔ **not a toast alone** |
| 📱 **Responsive behaviour** | **The key table scrolls inside its container** |
| **Definition of Done** | **A key can be issued, scoped, used, logged and rotated; a webhook can fail and be replayed** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Security Invariant** 🔴 **a key is stored as a hash and never in plaintext** · 🔴 **a key's scope is enforced by the same enforcer as a human's, never by a parallel check** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | 🔴 **This is the single largest security surface in the eight workflows** — ⛔ **and it arrived disguised as a settings section.** ✅ **Naming it as a platform row is the point of decomposing before implementation reaches it** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ⚠️ **UNDECIDED — [C-1404](LANE_COORDINATION.md) asks it.** ⛔ **Signing, rotation and delivery retry are commodity** ([497](architecture/497-commodity-and-differentiation.md)) |

### TASK-1414 · Workflow 23 · Security Center — a person's own security

| | |
|---|---|
| **Epic** | **Security Center** — **[workflow 23](coordination/workflows.toml)** |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** |
| **Status** | ⏸️ **`SEQUENCED` — behind [TASK-1410](#task-1410--step-up-authentication--the-capability-behind-stepup_required) and [TASK-493](#task-493--better-auth-plugins--four-capabilities-one-file)** — ⛔ **its controls ARE those plugins; without them the screen is decoration** ⚠️ **RE-CHECKED 2026-08-10 and CORRECTLY STILL PARKED.** ⛔ **[`state-check`](../infra/state-check.py) reported this row as waiting on a handed-over predecessor. It has TWO, and only one has moved: [TASK-493](#task-493) is `READY` — nobody has built it, so the Better Auth plugins this row's controls ARE do not exist yet.** ✅ **So the row stays `SEQUENCED` — releasing it would advertise work whose dependency genuinely does not exist.** 🔴 **The check appears to fire when ANY predecessor is handed over rather than ALL, which makes three of its four reports false positives — raised with QE, whose gate it is ([§20e](../CLAUDE.md)), rather than fixed here.** |
| **Design authority** | ⛔ **`docs/design-snapshot/screens/Fortune Signora Security Center.dc.html`** — 📁 *"Platform foundation · Module 6 · personal security"* |
| ✅ **What it delivers** | **§01 posture and open alerts** — 📁 ***"A device you have not used before signed in as you · This was not me"*** · **§02 password, including the SSO-managed case that sends the person to their provider** · **§03 second factors — TOTP enrolment as a four-step flow, and backup codes shown once** · **§04 recovery** — 📁 ***"There is no self-service reset for SSO accounts"*** |
| 🔴 **The sentence the row is really about** | 📁 ***"What Signora never does"*** — **the design has an explicit section stating the product's own limits.** ⚡ **That is the honesty principle ([C-017](LANE_COORDINATION.md)) rendered as a screen, and it must be built as content the product commits to, not as filler** |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | ✅ **`two-factor`, `passkey` and `multi-session` are Better Auth plugins [TASK-493](#task-493--better-auth-plugins--four-capabilities-one-file) registers** · ⚠️ **the sign-in history needs [TASK-492](#task-492--the-security-event-stream--one-append-only-record-every-module-writes)'s event stream** — ⛔ **it is a read of security events, not a new table** |
| **Reusable components required** | **[TASK-486](#task-486--workflow-3--email-verification-to-the-approved-design)'s OTP input** · **[TASK-485](#task-485--workflow-2--registration-to-the-approved-design)'s `PasswordField` and `PasswordStrength`** — ⛔ **both are already in `components/ui`** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`frontend/app/(app)/**`** · 🟠 **`lib/auth/server.ts` if a plugin is still unregistered — announced** |
| **Playwright coverage** | **A person enrols a second factor end to end and the backup codes are shown exactly once** · 🔴 **revoking a session actually ends it** — asserted from the revoked session, not from the list |
| **Component coverage** | ***This was not me* opens the full sign-in record** · **the strength meter REPORTS and never gates** — 📁 **[TASK-485](#task-485--workflow-2--registration-to-the-approved-design) established this and it must not be re-decided here** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | **Backup codes are readable, selectable and downloadable** — ⛔ **never an image** · **the enrolment stepper announces *step N of 4*** |
| 📱 **Responsive behaviour** | **The QR code and its manual key are both offered at every width** — 📁 the design's *"Cannot scan?"* is not a mobile fallback, it is always present |
| **Definition of Done** | **A person can see their posture, change their password, enrol and remove a second factor, and revoke a session** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Security Invariant** 🔴 **every destructive control on this page passes through [TASK-1410](#task-1410--step-up-authentication--the-capability-behind-stepup_required)'s challenge** — ⛔ **and the challenge is enforced server-side** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | ⛔ **Rendering the SSO-managed password state as a disabled field.** ✅ **The design sends the person to their provider with a working link; a disabled field is the false affordance class again** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **BUY — Better Auth, ratified** |

### TASK-1415 · Workflow 15 · Account 360 — the record page a rep works instead of the CRM

| | |
|---|---|
| **Epic** | **Account 360** — **[workflow 15](coordination/workflows.toml)** |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE, 2026-08-11, certified `fe33002`, promoted from `2f0e559`.** **Was:** 🔵 **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-11** (the measurement, the seven absent sections and the rail). 🔴 **The row's claim was measured rather than trusted**: 📁 *"each a composition of intelligence the platform ALREADY produces"* — ⚠️ **eight of fifteen sections have a read; seven have none.** ⛔ **The seven are RENDERED, not omitted** ([§20c](../CLAUDE.md)), each saying what it would show and what Signora cannot answer yet — **never *coming soon***. 📁 **[P-A](DOMAIN_DISCOVERY_REGISTER.md) one level up**: **absent is not empty**, and a rep who reads the second as the first concludes their account is quiet. ⚠️ **Three defects of mine, two found by LOOKING rather than testing** — platform vocabulary in customer copy · the reads live in three modules and I checked one · **the rail linked to ids only the absent sections carried**, so half its entries went nowhere. 🔴 **[B-237](BUGS.md) 🟠 found and fixed on the way**: a failed workspace read rendered *"you are not part of an organization yet"* — **P-A's worst instance, because it makes the customer's own membership look absent and invites them to create a duplicate.** ⏸️ **Named as remaining**: three supplied sections have no section on this page yet, and the seven absent ones render as a block rather than interleaved in the design's order. **2744 frontend passed · tsc 0 · eslint 0 · 3 journeys green, watched headed.** **Was:** 🔵 **`CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-10**, by the Task Claim Protocol. **Was:** ✅ **`READY` — Lane C, 2026-08-10.** ⚡ **Released because the COMPLETE predecessor set is now satisfied** — 📁 **[TASK-1408](#task-1408) `VERIFIED` and [TASK-1411](#task-1411) `PROMOTED`.** ⛔ **Fitness only, never priority** ([§20e](../CLAUDE.md)). |
| **Design authority** | ⛔ **`docs/design-snapshot/screens/Fortune Signora Account 360.dc.html`** — 📁 *"Flagship record page · everything a rep needs without opening the CRM"* |
| ✅ **What it delivers** | **Account score with its delta · customer health · buying intent · the signals timeline · a research summary · open opportunities** — ⚡ **each a composition of intelligence the platform ALREADY produces** |
| 🔴 **The reuse finding, and it is large** | ⚡ **Almost nothing here is new backend.** ✅ **[TASK-417](#task-417--account-360-renders-what-the-platform-already-knows) is titled *renders what the platform already knows* and is `IN VALIDATION`** — ⛔ **this row must begin by measuring what that delivered rather than rebuilding it**, which is [C-428](LANE_COORDINATION.md)'s rule and what [TASK-483](#task-483--the-authentication-shell) cost when it was skipped |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | **`buying_score_engine` · `signal_engine` · `opportunity_engine` · `timeline` · `ai_research_engine`** — ⚠️ **`timeline` publishes an EMPTY `public/` on `integration`** ([C-428](LANE_COORDINATION.md), reported by `state-check` every run) — 🔴 **so the signals timeline has no legal contract to cross by and that is this row's first blocker** |
| **Reusable components required** | **[TASK-1411](#task-1411--the-ai-attribution-primitive--one-confidence-one-source-list-one-show-reasoning)'s attribution strip** · **[TASK-1409](#task-1409--workflow-28--system-states--the-scope-ladder-and-a-failure-that-claims-the-smallest-surface-it-can)'s region boundary — 🔴 **eight independent sections on one page is the exact case the scope ladder exists for** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`frontend/app/(app)/accounts/[accountId]/**`** — ⚠️ **the route already exists and ships** |
| **Playwright coverage** | **The page renders with one section's data source failing and the other seven interactive** |
| **Component coverage** | **A score with no value renders absence, never zero** · **`On this page` navigation tracks the scrolled section** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | **Each section is a labelled `<section>` reachable by heading navigation** · **the score delta is spoken as *down 24*, not as a colour** |
| 📱 **Responsive behaviour** | **The `On this page` rail collapses to a select below `lg`** · **the opportunities table scrolls in its container** |
| **Definition of Done** | **A rep can answer *what is happening with this account* without opening the CRM** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `product_surface` + five engines through their `public/` · **Security Invariant** 🔴 **every section's read is workspace-scoped; a section that hides itself AFTER fetching has already leaked** — ⚡ **[D-196](DECISION_REGISTER.md) recorded this exact invariant: the check is on the READ, not the render** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | 🔴 **This page reads from five modules.** ⛔ **The temptation is a single aggregate endpoint, which couples five bounded contexts through one response shape** ([§5](../CLAUDE.md)) |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **n/a — Signora's differentiator** ([497](architecture/497-commodity-and-differentiation.md)) |

### TASK-1416 · Drill-down — any figure opens its own rows, and no number is dead

| | |
|---|---|
| 🔴 **A platform capability stated as a design principle** | 📁 **Reports: *"Any figure opens its own rows — no dead numbers"* · *"3 levels · scope preserved at each"*.** ⛔ **Preserving scope across three levels of drill-down is a query-composition problem, not a table** |
| **Epic** | **Reporting foundation** — serves **[workflows 19, 20, 21, 22](coordination/workflows.toml)** |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** |
| **Status** | ⏸️ **`SEQUENCED` — behind [TASK-1417](#task-1417--workflow-22--reports--built-once-read-three-ways)'s shared query**, whose output it composes |
| **Design authority** | ⛔ **`Fortune Signora Reports.dc.html` §*Drill down*** |
| ✅ **What it delivers** | **A figure carries the query that produced it**, so opening it yields **the rows behind that figure and no others** — 📁 the design's *"From: commit shortfall −$2.4M"* keeps the origin visible at every level |
| **Reusable components required** | ✅ **[TASK-449](#task-449--saved-view--the-public-contract-and-nothing-else)'s Saved View is the same idea one level up** — ⚠️ **a drill path is a transient saved view; the two must share a filter representation or they will disagree about what a scope IS** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`components/ui`** |
| **Playwright coverage** | 🔴 **Three levels deep, the rows shown are a strict subset of the level above** — **the assertion that makes *scope preserved* true rather than claimed** |
| **Component coverage** | **Breadcrumb back to any level restores that level's exact scope** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | **A drillable figure is a `button`, not a styled span** · **the drill breadcrumb is a `nav`** |
| 📱 **Responsive behaviour** | **Drill-down opens as a sheet below `md`, a panel above** |
| **Definition of Done** | **No figure in a report is inert, and three levels preserve scope** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Security Invariant** 🔴 **a drill-down re-evaluates permission at each level** — ⛔ **inheriting the parent's authorization is how an aggregate leaks its components** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | ⛔ **Implementing drill-down per chart.** ✅ **It is one contract — *a figure knows its query* — and per-chart implementations cannot preserve scope across levels** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **n/a** |

### TASK-1417 · Workflow 22 · Reports — built once, read three ways

| | |
|---|---|
| **Epic** | **Reports** — **[workflow 22](coordination/workflows.toml)** |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE, 2026-08-10, certified `57d6bc8`, promoted from `30d0c37`.** **Was:** 🔵 **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-10** (the definition, the three modes, the narrative merge, the picker and the route). 🔴 **The row's named risk was *"three renderers"* and it is refused by the TYPES**: a laid-out block carries the definition's own `ReportBlock` and **has no field into which a title, a figure or a sentence could be introduced** — asserted as a property across all three modes. ✅ **A report is stored as a saved-view declaration** — 📁 [455 §4](architecture/455-context-definitions-r02-r03.md) already names Reports among that store's consumers, **so no new table, module or endpoint was built**; Reports is the SECOND consumer, which is why the three refusals moved to a shared frame. ⛔ **FOUR capabilities named as absent, each measured**: scheduling · exports · sharing — **the row's own three** — **and GENERATING the narrative**, which the row did not name: 📁 `ai_platform` publishes exactly one endpoint, `account_narration`, scoped to an account. ⚡ **[C-283](LANE_COORDINATION.md)**, and [§10](../CLAUDE.md) forbids inventing one. ✅ **The merge that survives a regeneration IS built** — a law over sentences, needing no model. ⚠️ **A fire-test found one of my assertions DECORATIVE and it is recorded rather than quietly fixed**: *executive withholds every chart* passed with the rule removed, because its fixture had three non-charts and the cap of three excluded the chart anyway. 🔴 **The screen ships a designed empty state** ([§20c](../CLAUDE.md)) — **the design's `$42.8M` board pack is a query engine's output and there is no query engine.** ⚠️ **This lane first shipped it with NO rail entry and `reachability.py` refused that**, correctly. 🔴 **[B-233](BUGS.md) found by the HEADED review, not by the suite**: the navigation region failed on every authenticated screen at every width, with 2696 tests green. **Fixed, runtime-verified, fire-tested.** **2696 vitest / 138 files (+66, +4) · tsc 0 · eslint 0 · 4 Playwright journeys green.** **Was:** 🔵 `CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-10. **Was:** ✅ **`READY` — Lane C, 2026-08-10.** ⚡ **Released because the COMPLETE predecessor set is now satisfied** — 📁 **[TASK-1408](#task-1408) `VERIFIED` and [TASK-1411](#task-1411) `PROMOTED`.** ⚠️ **Held parked on 2026-08-10 earlier the same day because TASK-1411 was only `CLAIMED`; it has since been promoted, and the product owner's instruction was to release *when all* dependencies are satisfied, not to leave rows parked indefinitely.** ⛔ **Fitness only, never priority** ([§20e](../CLAUDE.md)). *(Was:* ⏸️ **⏸️ **`SEQUENCED` — behind [TASK-1408](#task-1408--the-application-shell--the-frame-six-bac***)* |
| **Design authority** | ⛔ **`docs/design-snapshot/screens/Fortune Signora Reports.dc.html`** |
| ✅ **What it delivers** | **A block builder over ONE shared query** — 📁 *"Every block inherits this"* · **an AI narrative that regenerates when the data moves and keeps the author's edits** · **screen, presentation and executive modes of the SAME blocks** — 📁 ***"never a separate deck to maintain"*** |
| 🔴 **The architectural claim inside the design** | ⚡ **Three modes, one source.** ⛔ **A separate presentation renderer is the thing the design explicitly refuses**, and building one would satisfy a screenshot review while creating the maintenance burden the sentence exists to prevent |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | ⚠️ **Scheduling, exports and sharing each need a capability that does not exist** — ⛔ **scheduled delivery is a job runner, export is a rendering pipeline, sharing is a link with its own authorization.** ✅ **Named here so they become rows rather than surprises; the first slice is the builder and the narrative** |
| **Reusable components required** | **[TASK-1416](#task-1416--drill-down--any-figure-opens-its-own-rows-and-no-number-is-dead)** · **[TASK-1411](#task-1411--the-ai-attribution-primitive--one-confidence-one-source-list-one-show-reasoning)** · **the chart library — 📁 *"8 types"*, and it serves workflows 19, 20 and 21 too** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`frontend/app/(app)/reports/**`** |
| **Playwright coverage** | **A report is built, presented and exported from one definition** · **an edited narrative survives a regeneration** |
| **Component coverage** | **Blocks reorder by keyboard as well as by drag** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | 🔴 **Drag-to-reorder MUST have a keyboard equivalent** — ⛔ **a drag-only builder is unusable without a mouse** · **every chart has a table equivalent** |
| 📱 **Responsive behaviour** | **Presentation mode is fixed-aspect and scales; the builder is single-column below `lg`** |
| **Definition of Done** | **One definition renders in three modes and the narrative survives regeneration** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Security Invariant** 🔴 **a shared link's authorization is evaluated at read time, never baked at share time** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | ⛔ **Three renderers.** ✅ **The design's own sentence is the acceptance criterion** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ⚠️ **The chart library is commodity — evaluate against [09-build-vs-buy.md](architecture/09-build-vs-buy.md) before writing one** |

|---|---|

**the design's subject is a QUESTION** scoped to a segment and a quarter — ⛔ **nothing in this product can be asked anything.** ⚡ **[C-284](LANE_COORDINATION.md)**, owner **Lane B**. ⛔ **Not built around, deliberately**: a `/research` route with a question box would be [§20c](../CLAUDE.md)'s failure in its worst form — **a control that invites the product's headline promise and cannot honour it.** ✅ **What the engine publishes already ships** — `ResearchPanel` carries the answer, its findings, the provenance and the `requested`/`running` states, **and already tells a failed read from *nobody has asked*** ([P-A](DOMAIN_DISCOVERY_REGISTER.md)). ⛔ **Rebuilding it would be the hybridisation [D-229A](DECISION_REGISTER.md) forbids.** ✅ **Delivered**: the capability measurement, and **the edit law extracted on its second caller** — 📁 both designs write the same sentence about edits surviving a regeneration, and `report-narrative.test.ts` passes **unchanged** against the moved law. **2751 frontend passed · tsc 0 · eslint 0.** **Was:** 🔵 **`CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-11**, by the Task Claim Protocol. **Was:** ✅ **`READY` — Lane C, 2026-08-10.** ⚡ **Released because its COMPLETE predecessor set is satisfied, checked rather than assumed**: 📁 **[TASK-1411](#task-1411) is `PROMOTED`, and it is this row's ONLY predecessor.** ⚡ **The AI attribution primitive is on `main`, so the thing this row renders exists.** ⛔ **Fitness only, never priority** ([§20e](../CLAUDE.md)) — the product owner owns where it sits. *(Was:* ⏸️ **⏸️ **`SEQUENCED` — behind [TASK-1411](#task-1411--the-ai-attribution-primitive--one-confidence-one-source-list***)* |

| ✅ **What it delivers** | **A research document with an outline, versions and comments** · **live progress while it runs** — 📁 *"Writing 'Recommendation' · 4 of 5 sections · 8.4s elapsed"* · **a conversation scoped to the research** · **and the knowledge graph of what it connected** |
| 🔴 **The product decision inside it** | 📁 ***"Editable · your edits are preserved through regeneration."*** ⛔ **That is a merge problem, and it is the hardest thing in the row.** ✅ **Named here so it is not discovered in the middle of a slice** |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | ✅ **`ai_research_engine` exists and [TASK-416](#task-416--an-evaluation-run-states-which-provider-served-it) established that a run states its provider** · ⚠️ **streaming progress needs a transport the platform does not have** — ⛔ **raised, not assumed** |

### TASK-1419 · Workflow 26 · Workflow Automation — simulated before it runs, approved before it writes, reversible after

| | |
|---|---|
| **Epic** | **Workflow Automation** — **[workflow 26](coordination/workflows.toml)** |
| **Module** | **`action_batch`** + **`product_surface`** |
| **Status** | ⏸️ **`SEQUENCED` — behind [TASK-466](#task-466--companies-bulk--the-first-consumer-end-to-end)**, the Action Batch's first consumer |
| **Design authority** | ⛔ **`docs/design-snapshot/screens/Fortune Signora Workflow Automation.dc.html`** |
| 🔴 **The reuse finding, and it is the largest in the eight** | ⚡ **The design's subtitle — *"Simulated before it runs, approved before it writes, reversible after it does"* — IS the Action Batch.** ✅ **[TASK-461](#task-461--the-action-batchs-public-contract-and-nothing-else)–[TASK-466](#task-466--companies-bulk--the-first-consumer-end-to-end) already built proposal, approval, an append-only log with no `UPDATE` grant, and asynchronous application.** ⛔ **This workflow is a SECOND CONSUMER of that machinery, not a new engine** — **and reaching it without this finding would have built one** |
| ✅ **What it delivers** | **A linear builder with branches only where a condition needs one** · **simulation as a dry run on live data with no writes** · **execution history and a step-through debugger** · **staged publishing** — 📁 *"Publish to 20% of scope"* |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | ✅ **`action_batch` is on `integration` with migration `0042` and both tables `FORCE`-RLS'd** · ⚠️ **simulation-on-live-data with no writes needs a read-only execution mode the module does not have** — ⛔ **that is this row's real backend work and it is one capability, not a subsystem** |
| **Reusable components required** | **[TASK-1416](#task-1416--drill-down--any-figure-opens-its-own-rows-and-no-number-is-dead)** for the simulation results · **the shared data table** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟠 **`backend/modules/action_batch/**`** — announced · 🟢 **the surface** |
| **Playwright coverage** | 🔴 **A simulation writes nothing** — asserted against the database, not against the UI · **a published workflow at 20% scope touches only that 20%** |
| **Component coverage** | **The debugger steps forward and back over a recorded run** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | **The builder is operable by keyboard end to end** — ⛔ **same rule as [TASK-1417](#task-1417--workflow-22--reports--built-once-read-three-ways)'s blocks |
| 📱 **Responsive behaviour** | **The builder is view-only below `md`** — ⚡ **a stated limitation is honest; a broken drag target is not** |
| **Definition of Done** | **A workflow can be simulated without writing, published to a fraction, and replayed** · **certified · `main`** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Security Invariant** 🔴 **a simulation runs under the INVOKER's permissions** — ⛔ **a dry run that reads more than its author could is a disclosure channel |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | ⛔ **Building a second execution engine beside the Action Batch.** ✅ **The design's three verbs map onto three capabilities that already exist** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **n/a — the machinery is already Signora's** |

| | |
|---|---|

has a source**: *a customer asks a question and gets an answer about their own workspace, with sources.* 📁 **[`app/(app)/help/answer.ts`](../frontend/app/(app)/help/answer.ts) composes it from [TASK-407](#task-407--b-i--a-connection-says-whether-it-actually-works)'s telemetry** — `health`, `attention`, `reason` — ⚡ **which is the exact distinction the design's own worked example turns on**: `ai_platform` holds the provider, **its `public/` is empty so no module may consume it**, and this row's budget is 🟢 `frontend/app/(app)/help/**` with no backend. ✅ **A derived answer that is correct beats generated prose that is plausible.** 🔴 **The row's named risk is met as an assertion**: ⛔ *shipping a generic documentation site* — **so a question naming no connected system returns NOTHING**, and ✅ **fire-tested**: making it answer anyway fails two tests, and inventing a cause the system did not give fails a third. ✅ **TWO OF THIS LANE'S OWN FINDINGS CLOSE**: 📁 [C-275](LANE_COORDINATION.md) — the top bar's help slot was absent *for want of a route* — **slot five is now both halves, help before account, always last** · 📁 [C-269](LANE_COORDINATION.md) — the rail's `Docs & support` — **now has its destination.** ⚡ **`reachability` is what surfaced it**: it refuses a route with no entrance, so the entrances arrived by measurement rather than by memory. ⛔ **FOUR design sections have NO SOURCE and are absent** — support entitlement · open tickets · region-scoped status · the documentation corpus. ⚠️ **Fourth instance of [C-1416](LANE_COORDINATION.md)'s pattern**, and ⛔ **a contractual response time is the worst possible thing to invent.** **2278 vitest / 113 files (+20, +1) · tsc 0 · eslint 0.** **Was: `CLAIMED` — Lane A, 2026-08-10** |

| ✅ **What it delivers** | **§01 help home with a workspace-aware answer and its sources** · **§02 system status scoped to the customer's REGION and integrations** — 📁 ***"not as a global average"*** · **§03 documentation with applicability** (*"Applies to Enterprise · Requires admin"*) |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | ✅ **[TASK-407](#task-407--b-i--a-connection-says-whether-it-actually-works) already makes a connection say whether it works** — ⚡ **that is the telemetry the worked example needs** · ⚠️ **an incident/status feed is an external dependency with no vendor ratified** — ⛔ **raised, not invented** |
| **Reusable components required** | **[TASK-1411](#task-1411--the-ai-attribution-primitive--one-confidence-one-source-list-one-show-reasoning)** — 📁 *"3 sources · answered in 1.4s"* is the same strip · **[TASK-481](#task-481--a-global-search-surface-composing-the-one-that-already-works)'s command palette — 📁 the design's `⌘/`** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`frontend/app/(app)/help/**`** |
| **Playwright coverage** | **A question about a broken integration returns an answer naming that workspace's own connection state** |
| **Component coverage** | **Status renders per region and per integration, never as one global figure** |
| ♿ **Accessibility** | **The answer is a live region announced once when complete** — ⛔ **not while streaming** |

| **Definition of Done** | **A customer asks a question and gets an answer about their own workspace, with sources** · **certified · `main`** |

### TASK-1408 · The Application Shell — the frame six backlog rows are waiting for

| | |
|---|---|
| 🔴 **Reuse — measured 2026-08-09, [D-214](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §8 conformance audit** | ⛔ **`components/ui/command-palette.tsx` EXISTS, is fully tested, and is mounted by NO production file.** 📁 **Its own comments already encode the design**: *"the palette never sorts, because the caller's order is the caller's ranking"* and *"⌘K opened it to 'Search or ask…' (§07), so the caller owns the query and the filtering."* ⚡ **Shell rule 7 — *anything clickable is reachable in the palette* — is this row's to satisfy**, and the primitive to satisfy it with is already built. ⛔ **Do not build a second one.** ⚠️ **This is the `confidence.tsx` near-miss repeating**: a complete orphaned primitive beside a row that needs exactly it |
| 🔴 **The fourteen shell rules are the acceptance criteria** ([D-210](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | 📁 **The design ends with a numbered constitution and it is what makes every later workflow consistent — read it before the screens.** **Modules own the content region and nothing else · no module may add anything to the top bar · one primary action per view, in the page header · global scope is read, never overridden · breadcrumbs mandatory below the first level · anything clickable is reachable in the palette · context that DESCRIBES goes in the utility panel, anything with a Save goes in a drawer · drawers never stack · at most two banners, environment before commercial · toasts confirm background outcomes only, never required reading · AI surfaces inherit the current scope and state it · errors render inside the shell unless the shell itself failed.** ⚡ **And rule 14 is the one this office is recording rather than restating** (📁 [D-137](DECISION_REGISTER.md) forbids a copied specification): ⛔ ***"Every new shell capability is an amendment to this document, not a local decision."*** 🔴 **A module that needs something the shell does not offer raises it — it does not add it.** ✅ **That single sentence is what stops thirteen remaining workflows each growing their own frame** |
| 🔴 **Why this exists, measured** | ⚡ **[D-201](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §5 asks *"what could stop Lane A three weeks from now?"*** ⛔ **Answered by scanning its own backlog: SIX of fifteen rows say *depends on an unbuilt workflow*, and every one of them means workflow 13.** 📁 **[TASK-481](#task-481--a-global-search-surface-composing-the-one-that-already-works) · [480](#task-480--the-notification-platform) · [482](#task-482--the-ai-copilot-entry-and-side-panel) · [471](#task-471--workflow-10--team-management-to-the-approved-design) · [1405](#task-1405--home-is-one-route-and-four-compositions) · [466](#task-466--companies-bulk--the-first-consumer-end-to-end)** |
| **Epic** | **Application Shell** — **[workflow 13](coordination/workflows.toml)**, and it had FOUR rows and no owning row |
| **Module** | **`product_surface`** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE, 2026-08-10.** ⚡ **Evidence rather than assumption: `git merge-base --is-ancestor` confirms every commit on `lane-a-product-surface` is contained in `main` at `346a0c4`, so this row's work has shipped.** **Was:** ✅ **`VERIFIED` — QE**, 2026-08-09, at `e72b6af0`. **Certified and promoted.** ⚡ **QE spot-checked the deliverables rather than accepting the row**: the shell components, `SystemState`, `RegionBoundary` and their suites are on `main`, and [D-215](DECISION_REGISTER.md)'s narrowing is recorded there too. ⚡ **Both rows were `BLOCKED` for four cycles on scope neither lane could clear** — **the narrowing, not the code, is what released them.** *(was `IN VALIDATION` — Lane A, 2026-08-09.)* ✅ **Released by [D-215](DECISION_REGISTER.md)'s narrowing of [C-257](LANE_COORDINATION.md), and the narrowed Definition of Done is MET.** ⚡ **Four placed regions, measured**: `ShellBanners` at `app-shell.tsx:79`, `Breadcrumb` in the top bar, the rail at `RAIL_WIDTH` with collapse per user and device class, and the top bar itself. ⛔ **The filter strip and the utility panel are out of this row.** ⚠️ **Shell rule 7 remains unsatisfied and is NOT silently dropped**: 📁 [C-259](LANE_COORDINATION.md) — the palette's opener is occupied by a working surface, and neither candidate row can mount it alone *(Was:* ✅ **`CLAIMED` — Lane A**, 2026-08-09, by the Task Claim Protocol. ⚡ **Taken in order**: the entry above it in [`queues/lane-a.toml`](coordination/queues/lane-a.toml) is [TASK-1409](#task-1409--workflow-28--system-states--the-scope-ladder-and-a-failure-that-claims-the-smallest-surface-it-can), which this lane already holds and whose remainder is blocked or sequenced — ⛔ **not a skip.** *(Previously* ✅ **`READY` — Lane C, 2026-08-09.** ⛔ **Assigned, not held** ([D-199](DECISION_REGISTER.md) §3). ⚡ **Independent: it needs nothing that does not exist**.*)**)* |
| **Design authority** | ⛔ **`docs/design-snapshot/screens/Fortune Signora App Shell.dc.html`** ([D-138](DECISION_REGISTER.md)). **No checklist, no restatement** ([D-137](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) |
| ⚡ **Delivers the frame, not its contents** | 📁 **The design's own words: *"Every module mounts inside this frame. No component is redesigned here."*** ✅ **Banners · top bar · breadcrumb · the rail** — ⛔ **and the design fixes their order: *"vertical order is fixed and non-negotiable"***, which this row honours for the regions it places |
| ⚡ **The filter strip and the utility panel are NOT in this row — narrowed 2026-08-09 by [D-215](DECISION_REGISTER.md), ruling [C-257](LANE_COORDINATION.md)** | ⛔ **This cell named all six regions, and two of them are unbuildable today for the same reason in two shapes: a control with no consumer.** 📁 **The strip serialises scope into the URL and no module reads scope; the panel's three tabs are module content and none exists.** ✅ **They are now [TASK-1432](#task-1432--the-filter-strip-behind-the-first-module-that-reads-scope) and [TASK-1433](#task-1433--the-utility-panel-behind-the-content-its-three-tabs-name), each `SEQUENCED` behind its own consumer.** ⚡ **[`lib/shell/scope.ts`](../frontend/lib/shell/scope.ts) already ships the narrowing law, which was the half that genuinely belonged here** |
| 🔴 **What it must NOT do** | ⛔ **Own navigation decisions.** 📁 **[D-193](DECISION_REGISTER.md)'s spec: *"modules register capabilities rather than issuing redirects"* — the shell CONSUMES [TASK-1400](#task-1400--the-authenticated-entry-engine--one-resolver-twenty-one-reason-codes)'s engine and does not contain one.** ⚡ **One rail item, `Home`, selected whenever the route is `/home` regardless of composition** — 📁 **the third of the four contradictions the spec resolved** |
| ✅ **Backend reuse — measured, not assumed** | ✅ **Nothing new.** ⚡ **The switcher reads membership `workspace_tenancy` already owns; search, notifications and Copilot are the four rows above and mount INTO this frame rather than being built by it** |
| **Reusable components required** | ⚠️ **The shell IS the reusable component** — ⛔ **every screen from workflow 9 onward mounts in it, which is why building it once beats fourteen screens improvising a frame** |
| **Shared layout** | ⚡ **This row is the shared layout.** ✅ **[TASK-470](#task-470--workflow-9--user-profile-to-the-approved-design) and [471](#task-471--workflow-10--team-management-to-the-approved-design) were written to deliver PANELS because it did not exist; they compose into it unchanged when it lands** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **`frontend/app/(app)/**`** · 🟠 **the shell container in `components/ui`** — **announced in the commit** · 🔴 **⛔ nothing: the frontend foundation freeze's UI-migration exemption covers this** ([§20c](../CLAUDE.md), [D-153](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) |
| **Playwright coverage** | ⚡ **A customer signs in and the frame renders with every banner in the design's fixed order** · **the switcher opens with `⌘⇧W` and finds a workspace** · 🔴 **and the assertion that matters: switching preserves the current route when the destination has it, and lands on Home when it does not** — 📁 **the design's own sentence, and *"it never drops the user on an error"*** |
| **Component coverage** | **Banner order is fixed** · **commercial banners are dismissible for the session and suppressed in sandbox; risky-state banners are undismissable** — 📁 **the design distinguishes them and the distinction is the test** · **system status is a PILL in the top bar, never a banner** |
| **Definition of Done** | **The frame renders with its four placed regions, every module mounts in it, and it owns no navigation decision** · **certified · `main`.** ✅ **Measured 2026-08-09, after the narrowing: `ShellBanners` at `app-shell.tsx:79`, `Breadcrumb` in the top bar, rail collapse per user and device class** — ⚡ **this row is reachable today, and it was not this morning** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `product_surface` · **Routes** the `(app)` layout · **APIs** membership read · **Playwright** the three scenarios above · **Security Invariant** ⛔ **the switcher must list only workspaces the caller is a member of — RLS asserted, not assumed** |
| ✅ **Verification strategy** | ⚡ **Six backlog rows becoming buildable is the verification.** ⛔ **If any of them still needs a shell of its own after this lands, the frame is wrong** |
| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | 🔴 **The temptation is to put routing in the shell** — it is where navigation visibly happens. ⛔ **The spec forbids it and [D-194](DECISION_REGISTER.md) found five modules already doing it; a sixth in the shell would be the hardest to remove, because it would look like the right place** |
| **Build-vs-Buy** | ✅ **n/a — Signora's own surface** |

| **Definition of Done** | **One append-only table, workspace-tenanted, registered in `tenancy_policy.py` in the SAME change — in `TENANT_OWNED_TABLES` *and* `IMMUTABLE_TENANT_TABLES`** ([§8](../CLAUDE.md)) · ⛔ **no `UPDATE` grant and no `UPDATE` policy — [§2](../CLAUDE.md)'s immutability is enforced by the schema, never by convention** · ⚡ **`membership_role_change` becomes its first PRODUCER rather than a second table** ([495 §6](architecture/495-principal-and-identity-boundary.md)) · **every event carries `subject_id` AND `acting_for`, both always** ([496 §3](architecture/496-security-architecture-blueprint.md)) — ⛔ **a line naming only the agent cannot answer *who authorised this*, and one naming only the human is a lie** · **certified · `main`** |
| **Module** | **`workspace_tenancy`** — ⚡ **it already owns `membership_role_change`, which is an audit row that does not know it** |
| ⚡ **Not a security log** | ⛔ **An *account activity* stream.** **A security-only log means building the second one later** — **compliance, support and the customer's own timeline all read the same facts** ([493 §5](architecture/493-security-platform-architecture.md)) |
| **Delivers** | **One append-only table, workspace-tenanted, no `UPDATE` grant and no `UPDATE` policy** *(the shape [TASK-463](#task-463) set for `action_batch`)* · **a typed `SecurityEvent` published through `public/`** · **the first producers: sign-in, sign-out, password reset, role change** |
| ⛔ **Out of scope** | **Any screen.** **Risk evaluation.** **Export.** ⚡ **A consumer appears when a workflow needs one** ([P-C](DOMAIN_DISCOVERY_REGISTER.md)) |
| **Consumed by** | **Login history · audit · compliance · risk signals · Team Management · Settings · Security Center** |
| ⚡ **Testing metadata** ([D-166](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **Modules** `workspace_tenancy` · **Routes** none · **Shared components** none · **APIs** none yet · **Playwright** none — ⛔ *not a customer-visible slice* · **Integration** a real-database test proving no `UPDATE` path exists · **Security Invariants** the append-only guarantee, and workspace isolation on the new table |
| **Change Budget** | 🟠 **A new table with its `tenancy_policy.py` registration, announced in the commit** |

|---|---|

| ⚠️ **Risk, named before it is met** | 🔴 **Registering `sso` changes what the sign-in card DOES** — 📁 **the design already draws it: *"northwind.com uses Okta. | **Definition of Done** | **Four capabilities registered and reachable, zero journeys changed** · ⛔ **the RS256 pin asserted unchanged** ([ADR-0026](architecture/adr/0026-jwt-boundary-architecture.md)) · **certified · `main`** |

colours light and dark, the radius scale at 4/6/8/12/16/20, elevations e1–e3, the shared `--ease` curve, and `--font-body`. ✅ **Archivo is loaded rather than only named** — `next/font` self-hosts it, Signora Design System v2.dc.html` at run time. **If the snapshot is refreshed and a value moves, it fails rather than agreeing with a copy.** ⚠️ **It caught two defects on its first run** — `--color-surface-sunken` was the page ground rather than the sunken surface, which would have made every sunken surface the colour of the page behind it; and one assertion of mine compared spelling rather than value. ⛔ **This is the check that would have caught [D-162](DECISION_REGISTER.md) before the product owner did**: every existing token test passed throughout that failure, because they finished foundation.** *(Previously* ✅ **`CLAIMED` — Lane A**, 2026-08-07, by the Task Claim Protocol. ⚠️ **Part of this row is already on `main`, and it got there before the row existed.** ⚡ **[D-162](DECISION_REGISTER.md) directed the token replacement to this lane directly** — *"if shared components currently expose the legacy visual language, update those shared components"* — **and `078fcf55` did it and was promoted in the same cycle Lane C wrote this row.** ⛔ **Recorded rather than tidied away**: a 🔴 change reached `main` on a product-owner instruction, and this row is its authorization arriving second. ✅ **What landed**: the warm palette light and dark, the radius scale at 6/8/12/16, elevations e1–e3, and the design's `--font-body` token — **taken from `Fortune Signora Authentication.dc.html`'s own `tokens()` function.** ⛔ **What has not**: this row names **`Fortune Signora Design System v2.dc.html` and its 38 tokens** as the authority, which is the fuller set — **and the Archivo face is not bundled**, so the font token resolves to the system stack. **Both are this claim's remaining work.** *(Previously* ✅ **`READY` — Lane C, 2026-08-07, on [D-161](DECISION_REGISTER.md).** ⛔ **It stands *above* [TASK-483](#task-483--the-authentication-shell) and [TASK-484](#task-484--the-two-journeys-that-work-and-nobody-verifies), and above every later workflow** — **[D-161](DECISION_REGISTER.md): *"Workflow implementations should inherit the finalized visual language automatically from these foundations rather than overriding styles on individual screens."***\)\) |

| ⚡ **What it delivers, measured in [489](architecture/489-legacy-ui-standards-audit.md) rather than guessed** | **① The sixteen colour values.** ⛔ **Zero of them are present today** — the palette is Tailwind's `slate` and `blue` ramps, which is Design System v2's own critique word for word. **② The radius scale, shifted one whole step**: `sm` is 4px where the design says 6, `md` is 6px where it says 8; `xs` and `2xl` are absent. **③ Four warm elevations** replacing three tinted `slate-900`. **④ `--ease: cubic-bezier(.32,.72,0,1)`** — no easing token exists. **⑤ Archivo** — ⛔ **the product declares no typeface at all.** **⑥ The eleven missing token roles**: `--font-body` `--ease` `--r-xs` `--r-2xl` `--bg-rail` `--ink-4` `--brand-ink` `--brand-line` `--ai-ink` `--ai-line` `--e4` |

| 🔴 **ADDED — extend `design-tokens.test.tsx`, in this same slice** | **The guard forbids hex colours, raw `rgb()/hsl()`, Tailwind palette classes, `dark:` variants, arbitrary `text-[…]` and `rounded-[…]`, and hardcoded control heights.** ⛔ **It does *not* forbid the three ways a screen can compensate for a foundation that does not fit: inline `style={{…}}`, `!important`, and arbitrary sizing or spacing — `p-[…]` `w-[…]` `h-[…]` `gap-[…]`.** ✅ **Add them.** ⚠️ **Deliberately NOT landed by this office ahead of the row**: the check fails today on the one `max-w-[400px]` above, and a gate that is red on arrival is a broken gate (§2). ⚡ **The token and the guard land together, in that order** |
| ⚡ **The measure of success, and it is falsifiable** | ⛔ **After this row, the previously rebuilt screens must inherit the finalized visual language with *zero* screen-level fixes.** **[D-162](DECISION_REGISTER.md): *"I expect previously rebuilt screens to inherit… automatically through semantic tokens rather than requiring screen-specific fixes."*** ✅ **If a screen still needs a fix, that is a foundation gap to name — not a screen to patch** |
| ⚠️ **What this does not fix** | ⛔ **Correct tokens on a screen missing elements is still a wrong screen.** **[488](architecture/488-workflow-1-login-gap.md)'s element gaps are [TASK-467](#task-467--the-authentication-surface-to-the-approved-design)'s and remain open** |

implementation: the design's `01 Welcome` needed a public root outside `(app)`** — ✅ **Lane A built it in Workflow 1 milestone 3, so this row inherits a solved problem rather than an open one.** ### TASK-479 · `people` publishes a way to read what it publishes

| | |
|---|---|
| **Discovery** | ⚡ **Lane C, 2026-08-07, content-checking [TASK-457](#task-457--people-publishes-what-other-modules-may-say-about-a-person) *before* releasing the rows behind it** — **the third time this week that opening a file changed a planning answer, and the first time it did so before the mistake rather than after** |
| **Module** | **`people/public/` — Lane B.** ⛔ **One module, one lane. No ownership moves** |
| **Status** | ⚪ **`DISPOSED` — superseded by [TASK-428](#task-428--m-i--what-the-domain-lets-other-modules-say), Lane C 2026-08-07.** ⚡ **Lane B delivered the port inside the row that needed it**, and its commit says so: *"two things that belong together: the use case that finally writes to the people tables, and the port another lane is blocked on."* ✅ **Verified by content**: `people/public/__init__.py` on `origin/lane-b-commercial-profile` is **6,899 bytes and exports `AffiliationReader(Protocol)`** alongside the two views — **that is this row's entire deliverable.** ⚡ **Creating it was right and disposing of it is right.** **When it was written nothing delivered a reader, [TASK-457](#task-457--people-publishes-what-other-modules-may-say-about-a-person) excluded one by name, and two rows were parked behind a contract they could not consume.** ⛔ **Lane B's slicing is better than mine**: `people` is its module, and shipping the port beside its first consumer beats a standalone row that would have needed its own claim, gates and handoff. ⚠️ **This office does not re-open it** — the work exists, and a row that duplicates delivered work is the [§2](../CLAUDE.md) defect of two representations of one truth |
| ⚡ **Trigger steward** | **Lane B** — it holds `people` in [`modules.toml`](coordination/modules.toml) |
| ⛔ **Why it exists** | **[TASK-457](#task-457--people-publishes-what-other-modules-may-say-about-a-person) says so in its own words: *"the types `people` publishes — a person, an affiliation interval — and nothing that reads them."*** ⚡ **That bound is deliberate and correct as a slice.** ⛔ **But [TASK-428](#task-428--m-i--what-the-domain-lets-other-modules-say) and [TASK-458](#task-458--m-h--the-people-a-customer-opens) both *consume* `people` data, and [§5](../CLAUDE.md) lets them cross only through `public/`** — **so when [TASK-457](#task-457--people-publishes-what-other-modules-may-say-about-a-person) integrates they will have the shape and no way to obtain one** |
| ✅ **Measured, not inferred** | **`people/public/__init__.py` on `origin/lane-b-commercial-profile` is 104 lines and exports exactly `PersonView` and `AffiliationView`.** ⛔ **`git grep -lE "class .*(Reader\|Writer\|Port)"` across `backend/modules/people/` returns nothing.** ⚡ **Compare [TASK-449](#task-449--saved-view--the-public-contract-and-nothing-else), the same repository's own precedent: it shipped `SavedViewReader` and `SavedViewWriter` alongside its views** |
| ⛔ **Not new scope** | **[TASK-428](#task-428--m-i--what-the-domain-lets-other-modules-say) is already approved and cannot be built without this.** ⚡ **A decomposition of approved scope is [§20e](../CLAUDE.md)'s definition of what Product Office may write** — *"decompositions of already-approved scope — never new scope, never a reordering."* **It is placed last and reorders nothing** |
| **Delivers** | **A read port on `people/public/`, and nothing else** — the shape [TASK-449](#task-449--saved-view--the-public-contract-and-nothing-else) already set for `SavedView`. ⛔ **No endpoint, no event, no domain change** |
| ⛔ **Scope bound, inherited** | **[C-221](LANE_COORDINATION.md), unchanged: no person's name anywhere.** **Role-based and organization-level only** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟠 **`people/public/`** — a module's own published contract, announced in the commit message |

| ⚡ **Why it is a separate row** | **It is the *consumer* half, and separating it is what keeps the boundary inside one lane each.** **[ADR-0403](architecture/adr/0403-signal-engine-reads-a-declaration.md) is the exact precedent — `signal_engine` reading another module's `public/`, one edge, no inner layer touched** |
| ⛔ **Do not start before its predecessor** | **A consumer written against an unpublished contract is the C-213 shape, and it is what this decomposition exists to avoid** |

### TASK-456 · The signal catalog explains itself to a customer

| | |
|---|---|
| **Discovery** | **[C-204](LANE_COORDINATION.md)** — *"the catalog explains nine words to nobody"* · [R5-04](architecture/39-product-review-findings.md) |
| **Module** | **`signal_engine` + Product Surface — Lane A** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE**, 2026-08-10, on `main`. **Previously:** **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane A**, 2026-08-06. ✅ **Shipped the same day it was claimed.** ⚠️ **The premise needed narrowing first**: **three of the catalog's four explanatory fields were already surfaced** — `hypothesis`, `limitations` and `suggested_validation` are on the validation surface, per account — **and exactly one reached nowhere: `why_it_matters`.** So the endpoint gains **one field and no more**, and the assertion pinning it to `{key, title}` is **widened rather than weakened**, with its original refusal now explicit in its own body. **Rendered for a screen reader rather than drawn**: the list is scanned before it is read (SW-05), and the place it is drawn is the account brief. **+3 tests, 1440 frontend, `mypy --strict` clean over 797 files, `make validate` PASSED.** *(Previously* **`CLAIMED`**, the moment [TASK-449](#task-449--the-saved-views-public-contract-and-nothing-else) reached `IN VALIDATION`. **Both modules are Lane A's.** ⚡ **It answers [C-204](LANE_COORDINATION.md)** — *"the catalog explains nine words to nobody"* — **and nothing is authored**: the explanations exist, are reviewed, and are simply not rendered. *(Previously* ✅ **`READY`** — **[D-116](DECISION_REGISTER.md)**.*)* |
| **Delivers** | **The catalog's own explanations, surfaced where the signal appears.** ⚡ **The knowledge exists and is reviewed** — §2: *domain experts own static knowledge*, and the catalog is that artifact. **Nothing is authored here; it is rendered** |
| ⚡ **[§0-D](PRODUCT_OFFICE.md)'s four questions, measured 2026-08-06 under [D-130](DECISION_REGISTER.md)** | ⛔ **① Reads — and the obvious one is the wrong one.** **`GET /workspaces/{id}/signal-catalog` returns *names only*, deliberately**, and its own docstring says why: *"What a hypothesis proposes, what it cannot prove and what to check are per-account questions the validation surface already answers; repeating them here would put the same prose in two places, and the copy nobody looks at is the one that goes stale."* ✅ **The explanations live on `GET /workspaces/{id}/accounts/{account_id}/validation`** — *"what it proposes, what it does not prove, and what to confirm before acting on it"* — **which reads `catalog.definition(signal_type)`, so it is the catalog's reviewed knowledge and not a second copy.** · **② Workspace-wide?** ⛔ **No — and this is the constraint the row must respect.** **The explanation read is *per-account* and *live hypotheses only*.** · **③ Writes** — ⛔ none, correctly · **④ Entrance** — ✅ **the account surface, where a held hypothesis already renders** |
| ⚠️ **Which surface, decided rather than left open** | ⛔ **This slice surfaces explanations *on an account*, where a signal actually appears — and it does not build a catalog browse page.** ⚡ **[C-204](LANE_COORDINATION.md)'s wording — *"the catalog explains nine words to nobody"* — reads as though a catalog *view* is wanted, and that reading is not buildable from the catalog endpoint as it stands.** **The journey in this row is the deciding evidence**: *a seller meets a signal they do not recognise* — **they meet it on an account.** ✅ **A catalog browse page remains possible and is not this row**; it would need `signal-catalog` to carry definitions, which is a deliberate design decision to reverse and therefore a slice with an owner, not a render |
| ⛔ **Out of scope** | **No model-generated explanation.** §10: *if the system already knows something deterministically, never ask a model to invent it again* — **and the catalog knows it** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟢 **A read of an existing catalog** |
| ⚡ **Playwright journey** ([D-113](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **A seller meets a signal they do not recognise and finds out what it means without leaving the queue** |

### TASK-453 · The six verbs become a published contract

| | |
|---|---|
| **Discovery** | **[C-419](LANE_COORDINATION.md)** · **[445](architecture/445-ai-copilot-traceability-audit.md)** — *"six verbs and no others"*, and *"17 surfaces, one contract"* |
| **Module** | **`ai_reasoning_engine` — its `public/`.** ⚡ **Ruled [D-112](DECISION_REGISTER.md): *"the engine owns the language. Screens consume that language."*** **§6: a port belongs to the module that owns the language it represents** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane B**, 2026-08-07. ⚠️ **Claimed 2026-08-06 and found unbuilt today** — `ai_reasoning_engine/public/__init__.py` was `__all__ = []` and `SUMMARIZE` appeared nowhere in the module on any branch. **The same defect class as [TASK-457](#task-457), found the same way — by content, not by a status string** ([C-235](LANE_COORDINATION.md)). ✅ **Delivered: `ReasoningVerb` and `Surface`, and nothing that uses them.** **The verb set is closed structurally** — a seventh cannot be added from outside the file — **and carries `SupportLevel`'s refusals**: no order, no number, equality kept for dispatch. **`Surface` is provenance and never an input**, with an AST scan that fails on any comparison against a surface outside its own constructor, **proved able to fail** against a synthetic violation. ⚠️ **`Surface` is deliberately open where `ReasoningVerb` is closed**: the six verbs are named in the design and **the seventeen surfaces are not named anywhere**, so the form is constrained rather than the membership — enumerating them would have been inventing architecture (§21). **+30 tests (160 in the module), `mypy --strict` clean over 832 files, 37 contracts, ruff clean.** ⛔ **No route and no request shape** — those are Epic G's, blocked whole on [C-334](LANE_COORDINATION.md). **Was:** ✅ **`CLAIMED` — Lane B**, 2026-08-06, by the Task Claim Protocol. [TASK-202](#task-202--epic-g--copilot) stands higher and is skipped on the credential ([C-334](LANE_COORDINATION.md)) — **not a priority judgement. Originally: `READY` — 2026-08-06** |
| **Delivers** | **The closed verb set — Explain · Summarize · Predict · Recommend · Generate · Reason — and a surface identifier, published through `public/`** |
| ⛔ **What the ruling forbids** | ***"Do not duplicate this taxonomy in frontend code."*** ⚡ **Put it in the frontend and the closed set stops being closed the first time a screen needs a seventh** |
| **Consumers** | **17 surfaces, as *consumers* rather than variants** — AI Copilot, Account 360, and every screen with an AI entry point |
| **Trade-off, stated** | **One route with a verb parameter is less RESTful than six routes.** **Accepted: the closed set is the product guarantee, and six routes make it six independently-extensible things** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟠 **A module's `public/`** |

| | |
|---|---|
| **Discovery** | **[R-02](DOMAIN_DISCOVERY_REGISTER.md)** — ⚡ *"measured as the highest-leverage capability in the product"* ([452](architecture/452-app-shell-traceability-audit.md)) · **[455 §4](architecture/455-context-definitions-r02-r03.md)** slice 1 |
| **Module** | **`workspace_tenancy` — Lane A.** **Ruled [D-091](DECISION_REGISTER.md).** ⚡ **The edge already runs**: `opportunity_engine/application/transition.py` imports `WorkspaceQueryPort` from `workspace_tenancy.public` today, **so no import contract changes** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE**, 2026-08-10, on `main`. **Previously:** **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane A**, 2026-08-06. ✅ **Shipped the same day it was claimed.** **Four types in `public/` and nothing else** — `SavedViewView`, `SavedViewListView`, `SavedViewReader`, `SavedViewWriter`. **No table, no endpoint, no implementation**, which is the deliverable rather than a staging post. **Every non-responsibility [455](architecture/455-context-definitions-r02-r03.md) names is in the types and held by a test**: the declaration is opaque *(a test fails if a `filters` or `sort` field ever appears)*, no results, **no `update` on the writer**, reader and writer separate, no events. **+10 tests, `mypy --strict` clean across 797 files, 36 import contracts kept, `make validate` PASSED.** *(Previously* **`CLAIMED`**, the moment [TASK-445](#task-445--stated-absence-becomes-one-implementation-instead-of-nine-local-ones) reached `IN VALIDATION`. ⚡ **This lane raised R-02's leverage as a blocking question on 2026-08-06 and it is now startable** — [D-112](DECISION_REGISTER.md) ruled the name, which was the whole of what held it. *(Previously* ✅ **`READY`** — released 2026-08-06 by [D-112](DECISION_REGISTER.md)'s ruling on [TASK-433](#task-433--segment-already-means-something-else).*)* |
| **Delivers** | **Four types in `public/`. No implementation, no table, no endpoint.** ⚡ ***"Unblocks every consumer immediately"*** — a consumer lane can compile, type-check and test against the port **before a row is persisted**, and `lint-imports` proves the coupling never forms |
| **The name** | ✅ **`SavedView`, ratified [D-112](DECISION_REGISTER.md).** ⛔ **`Segment` is not overloaded** — it stays `commercial_profile`'s ICP cohort |
| ⛔ **Deliberately absent** | **No event.** [P-C](DOMAIN_DISCOVERY_REGISTER.md) — *an event exists when a consumer appears, and none has.* **A context definition is exactly where a speculative event catalog would otherwise be born** · **No projection** — a saved view is the query, ***"not the results"*** |
| **Change Budget** | 🟠 **A module's `public/`.** No migration |

Its responsibility is persistence and retrieval, not execution."*** **A store that evaluates a filter has become a query engine, and that is the erosion this definition exists to prevent** |

### TASK-446 · An account's observed attributes have a publisher

| | |
|---|---|
| **Discovery** | **[C-332](LANE_COORDINATION.md)** *(Lane B)* · **[462](architecture/462-observed-attributes-proposal.md)** — the proposal, approved **exactly as written** by [D-111](DECISION_REGISTER.md) |
| **Module** | **`knowledge_graph`.** ⚡ **Chosen because it owns identity resolution, the `entity_id → SubjectRef` binding, the claims and the vocabulary** — *"no other module holds any of the four"* — ⚡ **assignee joined from [`modules.toml`](coordination/modules.toml), never stored here.** ⚠️ **This cell named `Lane B` until 2026-08-07; [D-165](DECISION_REGISTER.md) closed that lane and the registry says Lane A.** ⛔ **[C-213](LANE_COORDINATION.md)'s defect — an assignee copied into a row outranking the authoritative source** |
| **Status** | ✅ **`PROMOTED` — QE**, 2026-08-10, on `main`. **Previously:** ✅ **`IN VALIDATION` — Lane B**, 2026-08-06. **`AccountAttributesReader` published**, one call per entity, with `unknown` a first-class outcome and **no `SubjectRef` crossing the boundary** — asserted, not noted. The resolution rule is `select_name`'s and is **stated in the contract**, as [462 §3](architecture/462-observed-attributes-proposal.md) required. **The edge is narrowed to the inner-layer form**, the shape [ADR-0403](architecture/adr/0403-signal-engine-reads-a-declaration.md) used. **+14 tests; 3789 backend passed, coverage 95.37%.** ⚡ **This unblocks [TASK-406](#task-406--a-iv--an-icp-criterion-is-scored-against-outcomes), both halves.** **Claimed the same day — the lane that raised [C-332](LANE_COORDINATION.md) takes its answer.** [TASK-202](#task-202--epic-g--copilot) stands higher and is skipped on the reason in its own row — the hosted evaluation credential ([C-334](LANE_COORDINATION.md)) — **not a priority judgement.** **Originally: `READY` — Lane C, 2026-08-06, on the ruling** |
| **Delivers** | **`AccountAttributesReader` in `knowledge_graph/public/`** — **one call per entity, not three** — returning **the resolved value, the providers that agreed, and `unknown` as a first-class outcome** |
| **Dependency edge** | **`opportunity_engine → knowledge_graph.public`, and nothing else.** ⚠️ **`pyproject.toml` currently forbids `modules.knowledge_graph` to `opportunity_engine` *in full*** — **it is replaced with the inner-layer form, which is exactly the shape [ADR-0403](architecture/adr/0403-signal-engine-reads-a-declaration.md) used** |
| ⛔ **What it must never do** | **Never score, rank or weight a provider · never return a *best guess* without saying it is one · never expose `SubjectRef`** — **a provider identity crossing this boundary lets a consumer do resolution after all, which is the whole defect this closes** |
| ⬜ **Stated, not settled** | **Which resolution rule applies to an attribute value** — newest, most-corroborated, or declared precedence. ⚡ **Ship with the simplest defensible rule — most recent, with the agreeing providers named — *provided the rule is stated in the contract rather than buried in a query*** ([462 §3](architecture/462-observed-attributes-proposal.md)) |
| **Unblocks** | **[TASK-406](#task-406--a-iv--an-icp-criterion-is-scored-against-outcomes) — both halves** · **[R-00](DOMAIN_DISCOVERY_REGISTER.md) becomes usable rather than merely implemented, for 16 consumers** |
| ⚡ **Playwright journey** ([D-113](DECISION_REGISTER.md)) | **A seller saves a view, leaves the screen, returns, and finds it — then a colleague opens the same view and sees the same thing.** ⚡ **The second half is the point**: *per view, not per user* |
| **Change Budget** | 🔴 **A new cross-module dependency edge — and it is ruled.** **ADR required and now drafted against a decision rather than ahead of one** |

proceed on a legacy visual foundation. TASK-490 becomes the prerequisite for the remainder of the UI migration."*** ⚡ **A customer-visible row built before the palette moves is built twice** |
| ✅ **Delivers — the three concerns that are complete** | **Session and identity context** *(Better Auth is the sole authority; the Python tier verifies RS256 only)* · **Workspace context** *(`CurrentWorkspace`, membership verification, `SET LOCAL app.workspace_id`)* · **Search** |
| ⛔ **Out of scope, and each has its own reason** | **Notifications** — ⏸ [ADR-0040](architecture/adr/0040-notifications.md), deferred by [D-027](DECISION_REGISTER.md) · **AI entry points** — the *ask* route is missing, [C-419](LANE_COORDINATION.md) · **User preferences** — 🔴 nothing, ⚠️ **and deliberately *not* saved views** · **Feature flags · announcements · background activity** — [438 Epic J](architecture/438-platform-initiatives.md), deferred with triggers |
